<tr id="row432183615501"><td class="cellrowborder" colspan="2" valign="top" headers="mcps1.1.5.1.1 mcps1.1.5.1.2 "><a name="ol4214181712511"></a><a name="ol4214181712511"></a><ol id="ol4214181712511"><li>排序请务必按照字母序排列，以字典的格式，先英文，后中文。</li><li>第一列术语/缩略语名称需加粗。</li><li>请各个组件接口人帮忙确认，全称、含义和华为术语库是否一致？不一致的是否已和SE对齐，并上传到华为术语库。</li><li>不打任何的芯片标签，后续不再使用之后就淘汰。</li><li>评审通过后要求SE的确认邮件+上3MS库之后才能合入社区后台。</li><li>在最后一列“确认是否一致”，分为以下三种类型：<a name="ol5459142134716"></a><a name="ol5459142134716"></a><ol type="a" id="ol5459142134716"><li>是/确认与术语库已有定义一致。</li><li>已确认定义，待/已补充到术语库。</li><li>已使用定义对已有术语库进行纠错。</li></ol>
</li></ol>
</td>

</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row9721215101219"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="14.14%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.1 "><p id="p5034184"><a name="p5034184"></a><a name="p5034184"></a><strong id="b1094211163811"><a name="b1094211163811"></a><a name="b1094211163811"></a>AIPP</strong></p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="64.96%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.2 "><p id="p12772634183811"><a name="p12772634183811"></a><a name="p12772634183811"></a>Artificial Intelligence Pre-Processing，AI预处理。</p>
<p id="p5115727"><a name="p5115727"></a><a name="p5115727"></a>AIPP用于在AI Core上完成图像预处理，包括改变图像尺寸、色域转换（转换图像格式）、减均值/乘系数（改变图像像素），数据处理之后再进行真正的模型推理。</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.737895851381269%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.3 " style="display:none"><p id="p1214132919574"><a name="p1214132919574"></a><a name="p1214132919574"></a>CANN</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="5.111175281547791%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.4 " style="display:none"><p id="p1033722917571"><a name="p1033722917571"></a><a name="p1033722917571"></a>陈萍萍</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="7.440562133025313%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.5 " style="display:none"><p id="p780132911912"><a name="p780132911912"></a><a name="p780132911912"></a>否</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row172851654141414"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="14.14%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.1 "><p id="p2028595401416"><a name="p2028595401416"></a><a name="p2028595401416"></a><strong id="b1612415110153"><a name="b1612415110153"></a><a name="b1612415110153"></a>AIV</strong></p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="64.96%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.2 "><p id="p1928516545148"><a name="p1928516545148"></a><a name="p1928516545148"></a>分离架构硬件中的矢量计算（AI Vector）核。</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.737895851381269%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.3 " style="display:none"><p id="p1914112965712"><a name="p1914112965712"></a><a name="p1914112965712"></a>CANN</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="5.111175281547791%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.4 " style="display:none"><p id="p6337229165711"><a name="p6337229165711"></a><a name="p6337229165711"></a>牟楠</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="7.440562133025313%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.5 " style="display:none">&nbsp;&nbsp;</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row574554514012"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="14.14%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.1 "><p id="p8271031171318"><a name="p8271031171318"></a><a name="p8271031171318"></a><strong id="b32861139141316"><a name="b32861139141316"></a><a name="b32861139141316"></a>AMCT</strong></p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="64.96%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.2 "><p id="p12040165173"><a name="p12040165173"></a><a name="p12040165173"></a>Ascend Model Compression Toolkit，昇腾模型压缩工具。</p>
<p id="p12272315131"><a name="p12272315131"></a><a name="p12272315131"></a>是一个针对昇腾芯片亲和的深度学习模型压缩工具包，提供量化、张量分解等多种模型压缩特性，压缩后模型体积变小，部署到<span id="ph589221295311"><a name="ph589221295311"></a><a name="ph589221295311"></a>昇腾AI处理器</span>上后可使能低比特运算，提高计算效率，达到性能提升的目标。</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.737895851381269%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.3 " style="display:none"><p id="p114429125714"><a name="p114429125714"></a><a name="p114429125714"></a>CANN</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="5.111175281547791%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.4 " style="display:none"><p id="p1333872935720"><a name="p1333872935720"></a><a name="p1333872935720"></a>陈萍萍</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="7.440562133025313%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.5 " style="display:none"><p id="p14103407525"><a name="p14103407525"></a><a name="p14103407525"></a>否</p>
<p id="p1710390155215"><a name="p1710390155215"></a><a name="p1710390155215"></a>不一致，待刷新</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row2944204114402"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="14.14%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.1 "><p id="p15368184101819"><a name="p15368184101819"></a><a name="p15368184101819"></a><strong id="b66001411161810"><a name="b66001411161810"></a><a name="b66001411161810"></a>AMP</strong></p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="64.96%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.2 "><p id="p18963133211458"><a name="p18963133211458"></a><a name="p18963133211458"></a>Asymmetric multiprocessing，非对称多处理。</p>
<p id="p16963163214510"><a name="p16963163214510"></a><a name="p16963163214510"></a>一种多元处理的架构。在这种架构下，存在多个处理器；每个中央处理器在某个特定时间内，被指定一个特定的任务去执行。在对称多处理系统还没完全发展好之前，它曾被视为是一种软件的权宜之计，可以用来让多个处理器同时运作。在对称多处理系统开发好之后，现今它仍然被视为是一个较简单廉价的软件选项。</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.737895851381269%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.3 " style="display:none"><p id="p0465193561814"><a name="p0465193561814"></a><a name="p0465193561814"></a>MindCluster集群调度</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="5.111175281547791%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.4 " style="display:none"><p id="p63686416182"><a name="p63686416182"></a><a name="p63686416182"></a>瞿铁铮</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="7.440562133025313%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.5 " style="display:none"><p id="p1680142917199"><a name="p1680142917199"></a><a name="p1680142917199"></a>是</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row4289121714443"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="14.14%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.1 "><p id="p1978913535447"><a name="p1978913535447"></a><a name="p1978913535447"></a><strong id="b176001656164414"><a name="b176001656164414"></a><a name="b176001656164414"></a>AMP</strong></p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="64.96%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.2 "><p id="p12602110164512"><a name="p12602110164512"></a><a name="p12602110164512"></a>Automatic Mixed Precision，自动混合精度。</p>
<p id="p5790053114419"><a name="p5790053114419"></a><a name="p5790053114419"></a>AMP混合精度训练是深度学习中一种提高训练速度和效率的技术。它通过结合使用不同精度的数值格式来实现，通常包括浮点数的低精度版本（如半精度FP16）和高精度版本（如单精度FP32）。</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.737895851381269%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.3 " style="display:none"><p id="p2790853174411"><a name="p2790853174411"></a><a name="p2790853174411"></a><span>PTA</span></p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="5.111175281547791%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.4 " style="display:none"><p id="p179012538449"><a name="p179012538449"></a><a name="p179012538449"></a>刘倩</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="7.440562133025313%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.5 " style="display:none"><p id="p6790105313441"><a name="p6790105313441"></a><a name="p6790105313441"></a>是</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row16906181419120"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="14.14%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.1 "><p id="p1592179418"><a name="p1592179418"></a><a name="p1592179418"></a><strong id="b10943911163814"><a name="b10943911163814"></a><a name="b10943911163814"></a>AOE</strong></p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="64.96%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.2 "><p id="p359617124112"><a name="p359617124112"></a><a name="p359617124112"></a>Ascend Optimization Engine，昇腾调优引擎。</p>
<p id="p205981714414"><a name="p205981714414"></a><a name="p205981714414"></a>用于封装调优过程中的ATC编译及AscendCL运行服务接口，提供并行调优功能。</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.737895851381269%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.3 " style="display:none"><p id="p1414162913576"><a name="p1414162913576"></a><a name="p1414162913576"></a>CANN</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="5.111175281547791%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.4 " style="display:none"><p id="p73381129175720"><a name="p73381129175720"></a><a name="p73381129175720"></a>刘晓芳</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="7.440562133025313%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.5 " style="display:none"><p id="p178010295193"><a name="p178010295193"></a><a name="p178010295193"></a>是</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row107531632172716"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="14.14%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.1 "><p id="p0753432172712"><a name="p0753432172712"></a><a name="p0753432172712"></a><strong id="b697013712717"><a name="b697013712717"></a><a name="b697013712717"></a>AOL</strong></p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="64.96%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.2 "><p id="p18753153213276"><a name="p18753153213276"></a><a name="p18753153213276"></a>Ascend Operator Library，昇腾算子库。</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.737895851381269%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.3 " style="display:none"><p id="p414429145718"><a name="p414429145718"></a><a name="p414429145718"></a>CANN</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="5.111175281547791%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.4 " style="display:none"><p id="p2338029185713"><a name="p2338029185713"></a><a name="p2338029185713"></a>陈娇</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="7.440562133025313%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.5 " style="display:none"><p id="p28011229151910"><a name="p28011229151910"></a><a name="p28011229151910"></a>是</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row19373171616434"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="14.14%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.1 "><p id="p169641532124510"><a name="p169641532124510"></a><a name="p169641532124510"></a><strong id="b5964173212454"><a name="b5964173212454"></a><a name="b5964173212454"></a>ARM</strong></p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="64.96%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.2 "><p id="p0964163294520"><a name="p0964163294520"></a><a name="p0964163294520"></a>Advanced RISC Machine，高级精简指令集机器。</p>
<p id="p51971333193019"><a name="p51971333193019"></a><a name="p51971333193019"></a>ARM处理器是Acorn有限公司面向低预算市场设计的第一款RISC微处理器。全称为Acorn RISC Machine。ARM处理器本身是32位设计，但也配备16位指令集，一般来讲比等价32位代码节省达35%，却能保留32位系统的所有优势。</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.737895851381269%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.3 " style="display:none"><p id="p36904912551"><a name="p36904912551"></a><a name="p36904912551"></a>MindCluster集群调度</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="5.111175281547791%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.4 " style="display:none"><p id="p569349155518"><a name="p569349155518"></a><a name="p569349155518"></a>瞿铁铮</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="7.440562133025313%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.5 " style="display:none"><p id="p118021629141915"><a name="p118021629141915"></a><a name="p118021629141915"></a>是</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row129191454104216"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="14.14%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.1 "><p id="p1015413286493"><a name="p1015413286493"></a><a name="p1015413286493"></a><strong id="b5445143444916"><a name="b5445143444916"></a><a name="b5445143444916"></a>ARP</strong></p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="64.96%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.2 "><p id="p1154132824913"><a name="p1154132824913"></a><a name="p1154132824913"></a>Address Resolution Protocol，地址解析协议。</p>
<p id="p12716363425"><a name="p12716363425"></a><a name="p12716363425"></a>将IP地址映射为MAC地址的互联网协议，允许主机和路由器通过ARP请求和ARP回应确定链路层地址。</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.737895851381269%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.3 " style="display:none"><p id="p1154728104915"><a name="p1154728104915"></a><a name="p1154728104915"></a>HDK</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="5.111175281547791%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.4 " style="display:none"><p id="p131544286491"><a name="p131544286491"></a><a name="p131544286491"></a>陈先雨</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="7.440562133025313%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.5 " style="display:none"><p id="p178025291192"><a name="p178025291192"></a><a name="p178025291192"></a>是</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row179031244382"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="14.14%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.1 "><p id="p1690513348912"><a name="p1690513348912"></a><a name="p1690513348912"></a><strong id="b1533132118343"><a name="b1533132118343"></a><a name="b1533132118343"></a>AscendCL</strong></p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="64.96%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.2 "><p id="p7486131510359"><a name="p7486131510359"></a><a name="p7486131510359"></a>Ascend Computing Language，昇腾编程语言。</p>
<p id="p189014718431"><a name="p189014718431"></a><a name="p189014718431"></a>提供运行时管理、单算子调用、模型推理、媒体数据处理等API，能够实现利用底层硬件计算资源，在CANN平台上进行深度学习推理计算、图形图像预处理、单算子加速计算等。</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.737895851381269%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.3 " style="display:none"><p id="p18149290571"><a name="p18149290571"></a><a name="p18149290571"></a>CANN</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="5.111175281547791%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.4 " style="display:none"><p id="p18337112912572"><a name="p18337112912572"></a><a name="p18337112912572"></a>叶成美</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="7.440562133025313%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.5 " style="display:none"><p id="p38014297192"><a name="p38014297192"></a><a name="p38014297192"></a>是</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row15571195545415"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="14.14%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.1 "><p id="p29861921155817"><a name="p29861921155817"></a><a name="p29861921155817"></a><strong id="b14944811193813"><a name="b14944811193813"></a><a name="b14944811193813"></a>Ascend EP</strong></p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="64.96%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.2 "><p id="p549322910367"><a name="p549322910367"></a><a name="p549322910367"></a>Ascend Endpoint，<span id="ph9242172895510"><a name="ph9242172895510"></a><a name="ph9242172895510"></a>昇腾AI处理器</span>作为终端节点（从控节点）。</p>
<p id="p338314012518"><a name="p338314012518"></a><a name="p338314012518"></a>以<span id="ph1884112108250"><a name="ph1884112108250"></a><a name="ph1884112108250"></a>昇腾AI处理器</span>的PCIe的工作模式进行区分，如果PCIe工作在从模式，则称为Ascend EP。</p>
<p id="p1998682110586"><a name="p1998682110586"></a><a name="p1998682110586"></a>通常由Host侧作为主端，Device侧作为从端（即EP）。客户的AI业务程序运行在Host系统中，<span id="ph10668165872510"><a name="ph10668165872510"></a><a name="ph10668165872510"></a>昇腾AI处理器</span>作为Device系统以PCIe从设备接入Host系统，Host系统通过PCIe通道与Device系统交互，将AI任务加载到Device侧的<span id="ph129021410310"><a name="ph129021410310"></a><a name="ph129021410310"></a>昇腾AI处理器</span>中运行。<span>Device通过PCIe通道配合主设备（X86、ARM等各种服务器）进行工作，Device只为Host提供NN计算能力，且Device上的CPU资源仅能通过Host调用</span>。</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.737895851381269%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.3 " style="display:none"><p id="p105711155175419"><a name="p105711155175419"></a><a name="p105711155175419"></a>CANN</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="5.111175281547791%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.4 " style="display:none"><p id="p357145517543"><a name="p357145517543"></a><a name="p357145517543"></a>刘晓芳</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="7.440562133025313%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.5 " style="display:none"><p id="p1572195585413"><a name="p1572195585413"></a><a name="p1572195585413"></a>是</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row99401330203417"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="14.14%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.1 "><p id="p149411830163412"><a name="p149411830163412"></a><a name="p149411830163412"></a><strong id="b7677174953412"><a name="b7677174953412"></a><a name="b7677174953412"></a>Ascend IR</strong></p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="64.96%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.2 "><p id="p18278153423413"><a name="p18278153423413"></a><a name="p18278153423413"></a>Ascend Intermediate Representation，昇腾AI处理器专用的、用于表达计算流程的抽象数据结构。在昇腾文档中，若无特殊说明，IR默认指代Ascend IR。</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.737895851381269%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.3 " style="display:none"><p id="p237142943520"><a name="p237142943520"></a><a name="p237142943520"></a>CANN</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="5.111175281547791%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.4 " style="display:none"><p id="p1285832163520"><a name="p1285832163520"></a><a name="p1285832163520"></a>牟楠</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="7.440562133025313%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.5 " style="display:none"><p id="p1938152973514"><a name="p1938152973514"></a><a name="p1938152973514"></a>否待录入</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row3525105715548"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="14.14%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.1 "><p id="p9664114015579"><a name="p9664114015579"></a><a name="p9664114015579"></a><strong id="b1994514113383"><a name="b1994514113383"></a><a name="b1994514113383"></a>Ascend RC</strong></p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="64.96%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.2 "><p id="p1440393563611"><a name="p1440393563611"></a><a name="p1440393563611"></a>Ascend Root Complex，<span id="ph151231636165515"><a name="ph151231636165515"></a><a name="ph151231636165515"></a>昇腾AI处理器</span>作为根组件（主控节点）。</p>
<p id="p165211403272"><a name="p165211403272"></a><a name="p165211403272"></a>以<span id="ph1325374112275"><a name="ph1325374112275"></a><a name="ph1325374112275"></a>昇腾AI处理器</span>的PCIe的工作模式进行区分，如果PCIe工作在主模式，则称为Ascend RC。</p>
<p id="p166454035719"><a name="p166454035719"></a><a name="p166454035719"></a>产品的CPU直接运行用户指定的AI业务软件，网络摄像头、I<sup id="zh-cn_topic_0000001687495573_sup550092143115"><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001687495573_sup550092143115"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001687495573_sup550092143115"></a>2</sup>C传感器、SPI显示器等其他外挂设备作为从设备接入产品。</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.737895851381269%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.3 " style="display:none"><p id="p4525155715411"><a name="p4525155715411"></a><a name="p4525155715411"></a>CANN</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="5.111175281547791%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.4 " style="display:none"><p id="p05261576547"><a name="p05261576547"></a><a name="p05261576547"></a>刘晓芳</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="7.440562133025313%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.5 " style="display:none"><p id="p952645717544"><a name="p952645717544"></a><a name="p952645717544"></a>是</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row1160582361619"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="14.14%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.1 "><p id="p4332457565"><a name="p4332457565"></a><a name="p4332457565"></a><strong id="b113347407592"><a name="b113347407592"></a><a name="b113347407592"></a>ASLR</strong></p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="64.96%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.2 "><p id="p1332105105620"><a name="p1332105105620"></a><a name="p1332105105620"></a>Address Space Layout Randomization，内存地址随机化机制。</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.737895851381269%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.3 " style="display:none"><p id="p5227643181611"><a name="p5227643181611"></a><a name="p5227643181611"></a>MindCluster集群调度</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="5.111175281547791%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.4 " style="display:none"><p id="p15228343201613"><a name="p15228343201613"></a><a name="p15228343201613"></a>瞿铁铮</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="7.440562133025313%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.5 " style="display:none"><p id="p480172941919"><a name="p480172941919"></a><a name="p480172941919"></a>是</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row10711142817438"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="14.14%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.1 "><p id="p5994149142119"><a name="p5994149142119"></a><a name="p5994149142119"></a><strong id="b101516595217"><a name="b101516595217"></a><a name="b101516595217"></a>ATB</strong></p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="64.96%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.2 "><p id="p7994174915215"><a name="p7994174915215"></a><a name="p7994174915215"></a>Ascend Transformer Boost，是一款基于昇腾AI处理器，专门为Transformer模型的训练和推理而设计的加速库。</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.737895851381269%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.3 " style="display:none"><p id="p19942049142113"><a name="p19942049142113"></a><a name="p19942049142113"></a><span>CANN</span></p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="5.111175281547791%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.4 " style="display:none"><p id="p13994249172117"><a name="p13994249172117"></a><a name="p13994249172117"></a>杨露露</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="7.440562133025313%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.5 " style="display:none"><p id="p1980272919197"><a name="p1980272919197"></a><a name="p1980272919197"></a>是</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row13294581018"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="14.14%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.1 "><p id="p439610015512"><a name="p439610015512"></a><a name="p439610015512"></a><strong id="b12946161193819"><a name="b12946161193819"></a><a name="b12946161193819"></a><span>ATC</span></strong></p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="64.96%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.2 "><p id="p113978012518"><a name="p113978012518"></a><a name="p113978012518"></a><span>Ascend Tensor Compiler</span>，昇腾张量编译器。</p>
<p id="p1433125815471"><a name="p1433125815471"></a><a name="p1433125815471"></a><span>是异构计算架构</span>CANN体系下的模型转换工具，它可以将开源框架的网络模型以及Ascend IR定义的单算子描述文件（json格式）转换为<span id="ph19331115811471"><a name="ph19331115811471"></a><a name="ph19331115811471"></a>昇腾AI处理器</span>支持的.om格式离线模型。模型转换过程中，ATC会进行算子调度优化、权重数据重排、内存使用优化等具体操作，对原始的深度学习模型进行进一步的调优，从而满足部署场景下的高性能需求，使其能够高效执行在<span id="ph1733115874717"><a name="ph1733115874717"></a><a name="ph1733115874717"></a>昇腾AI处理器</span>上。</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.737895851381269%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.3 " style="display:none"><p id="p121482985714"><a name="p121482985714"></a><a name="p121482985714"></a>CANN</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="5.111175281547791%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.4 " style="display:none"><p id="p1833842910573"><a name="p1833842910573"></a><a name="p1833842910573"></a>陈萍萍</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="7.440562133025313%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.5 " style="display:none"><p id="p3932932125114"><a name="p3932932125114"></a><a name="p3932932125114"></a>否</p>
<p id="p1580112971910"><a name="p1580112971910"></a><a name="p1580112971910"></a>不一致，待刷新</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row51371054185219"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="14.14%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.1 "><p id="p6873115565218"><a name="p6873115565218"></a><a name="p6873115565218"></a><strong id="b225319411531"><a name="b225319411531"></a><a name="b225319411531"></a>AVI</strong></p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="64.96%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.2 "><p id="p6413183985415"><a name="p6413183985415"></a><a name="p6413183985415"></a>Ascend Virtual Instance，昇腾虚拟化实例。</p>
<p id="p387365511522"><a name="p387365511522"></a><a name="p387365511522"></a>指通过资源虚拟化技术将1个NPU切分成若干个vNPU（虚拟NPU）实例挂载到虚拟机或容器中使用，使得1个NPU同时支持多个计算调度任务。AVI通过算力资源切分的方式，实现了资源虚拟化复用并保证了资源的安全隔离，大大降低了用户使用NPU算力的门槛和成本。并且支持多租户按需进行资源申请或回收管理。</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.737895851381269%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.3 " style="display:none"><p id="p98731555165210"><a name="p98731555165210"></a><a name="p98731555165210"></a>HDK</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="5.111175281547791%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.4 " style="display:none"><p id="p18873055165214"><a name="p18873055165214"></a><a name="p18873055165214"></a>陈先雨</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="7.440562133025313%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.5 " style="display:none"><p id="p7873105505216"><a name="p7873105505216"></a><a name="p7873105505216"></a>是</p>
</td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>

<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="7.519248075192481%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.5 " style="display:none"><p id="p1870219813206"><a name="p1870219813206"></a><a name="p1870219813206"></a>是</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row771373811391"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="14.14%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.1 "><p id="p1272826"><a name="p1272826"></a><a name="p1272826"></a><strong id="b55351160386"><a name="b55351160386"></a><a name="b55351160386"></a>BIU</strong></p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="64.96%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.2 "><p id="p35990092"><a name="p35990092"></a><a name="p35990092"></a>Bus Interface Unit，总线接口单元。AI Core与总线交互的接口。</p>
</td>

</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="7.519248075192481%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.5 " style="display:none"><p id="p1702128172015"><a name="p1702128172015"></a><a name="p1702128172015"></a>否</p>
<p id="p2792812317"><a name="p2792812317"></a><a name="p2792812317"></a>需要补充到术语库</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row5593225775"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="14.14%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.1 "><p id="p36016269465"><a name="p36016269465"></a><a name="p36016269465"></a><strong id="b060142614611"><a name="b060142614611"></a><a name="b060142614611"></a>BIOS</strong></p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="64.96%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.2 "><p id="p2060132624619"><a name="p2060132624619"></a><a name="p2060132624619"></a>Basic Input Output System，基本输入输出系统。</p>
<p id="p17601202614614"><a name="p17601202614614"></a><a name="p17601202614614"></a>存于计算机主板上的一种固件，包括基本输入输出控制程序、上电自检程序、系统启动自举程序、系统设置信息，为计算机提供底层的硬件设置和控制功能。</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.48935106489351%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.3 " style="display:none"><p id="p6593162510712"><a name="p6593162510712"></a><a name="p6593162510712"></a>MindCluster</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.95930406959304%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.4 " style="display:none"><p id="p9593162511718"><a name="p9593162511718"></a><a name="p9593162511718"></a>胡可茹</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="7.519248075192481%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.5 " style="display:none"><p id="p14702178102013"><a name="p14702178102013"></a><a name="p14702178102013"></a>是</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row165426290314"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="14.14%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.1 "><p id="p7501194742812"><a name="p7501194742812"></a><a name="p7501194742812"></a><strong id="b2049716455316"><a name="b2049716455316"></a><a name="b2049716455316"></a>BLAS</strong></p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="64.96%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.2 "><p id="p45011547142817"><a name="p45011547142817"></a><a name="p45011547142817"></a>Basic Linear Algebra Subprograms，基础线性代数子程序。</p>
<p id="p1025811842318"><a name="p1025811842318"></a><a name="p1025811842318"></a>基本线性代数子程序（BLAS）为高性能计算提供了一套方便的算法软件工具。</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.48935106489351%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.3 " style="display:none"><p id="p105421929113115"><a name="p105421929113115"></a><a name="p105421929113115"></a>CANN信号加速库</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.95930406959304%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.4 " style="display:none"><p id="p154214296314"><a name="p154214296314"></a><a name="p154214296314"></a>冯英</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="7.519248075192481%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.5 " style="display:none"><p id="p7542182916317"><a name="p7542182916317"></a><a name="p7542182916317"></a>是</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row168171936183519"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="14.14%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.1 "><p id="p167131958119"><a name="p167131958119"></a><a name="p167131958119"></a><strong id="b10229186821"><a name="b10229186821"></a><a name="b10229186821"></a>BOM</strong></p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="64.96%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.2 "><p id="p197145586120"><a name="p197145586120"></a><a name="p197145586120"></a>Bill of Materials，物料清单。</p>
<p id="p17813217134311"><a name="p17813217134311"></a><a name="p17813217134311"></a>为了制造最终产品所使用的文件，内容记载原物料清单、主/副加工流程、各部位明细、半成品与成品数量等资讯。通常作为代工双方联系的文件或是公司内部沟通的文件。</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.48935106489351%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.3 " style="display:none"><p id="p37141658411"><a name="p37141658411"></a><a name="p37141658411"></a>HDK</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.95930406959304%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.4 " style="display:none"><p id="p18714758611"><a name="p18714758611"></a><a name="p18714758611"></a>陈先雨</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="7.519248075192481%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.5 " style="display:none"><p id="p1670388142016"><a name="p1670388142016"></a><a name="p1670388142016"></a>是</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row330641210517"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="14.14%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.1 "><p id="p1724514316150"><a name="p1724514316150"></a><a name="p1724514316150"></a><strong id="b129942005713"><a name="b129942005713"></a><a name="b129942005713"></a>BP Point</strong></p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="64.96%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.2 "><p id="p17245831161516"><a name="p17245831161516"></a><a name="p17245831161516"></a>Back Propagation Point，训练网络迭代轨迹反向算子的结束位置。</p>
</td>

<tr id="row146651414184015"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="14.14%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.1 "><p id="p166961491856"><a name="p166961491856"></a><a name="p166961491856"></a><strong id="b79941636408"><a name="b79941636408"></a><a name="b79941636408"></a>CANN</strong></p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="64.96%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.2 "><p id="p988385814515"><a name="p988385814515"></a><a name="p988385814515"></a>Compute Architecture for Neural Networks，异构计算架构。</p>
<p id="p1288315811510"><a name="p1288315811510"></a><a name="p1288315811510"></a>CANN是昇腾针对AI场景推出的异构计算架构，对上支持多种AI框架，对下服务AI处理器与编程，发挥承上启下的关键作用，是提升昇腾AI处理器计算效率的关键平台。同时针对多样化应用场景，提供高效易用的编程接口，支持用户快速构建基于昇腾平台的AI应用和业务。</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="7.23%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.3 " style="display:none"><p id="p247175033618"><a name="p247175033618"></a><a name="p247175033618"></a>CANN</p>
</td>

</td>

</td>
</tr>

</td>

</td>

</td>
</tr>

<tr id="row1466671417406"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="14.14%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.1 "><p id="p1408093"><a name="p1408093"></a><a name="p1408093"></a><strong id="b6998123134015"><a name="b6998123134015"></a><a name="b6998123134015"></a>CNN</strong></p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="64.96%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.2 "><p id="p46946741"><a name="p46946741"></a><a name="p46946741"></a>Convolutional Neural Network，卷积神经网络。</p>
<p id="p54992765"><a name="p54992765"></a><a name="p54992765"></a>是一种前馈神经网络，人工神经元可以响应周围单元，可以进行大型图像处理。</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="7.23%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.3 " style="display:none"><p id="p15471650143617"><a name="p15471650143617"></a><a name="p15471650143617"></a>CANN</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.43%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.4 " style="display:none"><p id="p14771369432"><a name="p14771369432"></a><a name="p14771369432"></a>陈娇</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="7.5200000000000005%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.5 " style="display:none"><p id="p057482932018"><a name="p057482932018"></a><a name="p057482932018"></a>是</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row196661914154013"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="14.14%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.1 "><p id="p20685388194"><a name="p20685388194"></a><a name="p20685388194"></a><strong id="b125710542237"><a name="b125710542237"></a><a name="b125710542237"></a>Cosine Similarity</strong></p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="64.96%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.2 "><p id="p1869354352413"><a name="p1869354352413"></a><a name="p1869354352413"></a>余弦相似度算法。</p>
<p id="p7395859135918"><a name="p7395859135918"></a><a name="p7395859135918"></a>精度比对算法之一，计算结果取值范围为[-1,1]，比对的结果如果越接近1，表示两者的值越相近，越接近-1意味着两者的值越相反。</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="7.23%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.3 " style="display:none"><p id="p1447125013364"><a name="p1447125013364"></a><a name="p1447125013364"></a>MindStudio</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.43%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.4 " style="display:none"><p id="p977236104318"><a name="p977236104318"></a><a name="p977236104318"></a>蔡伟伟</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="7.5200000000000005%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.5 " style="display:none"><p id="p10574029162010"><a name="p10574029162010"></a><a name="p10574029162010"></a>否，待加入术语库</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row46679145409"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="14.14%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.1 "><p id="p14197042"><a name="p14197042"></a><a name="p14197042"></a><strong id="b1501546408"><a name="b1501546408"></a><a name="b1501546408"></a>Cube</strong></p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="64.96%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.2 "><p id="p63631028135120"><a name="p63631028135120"></a><a name="p63631028135120"></a>AI Core上的Cube计算单元，负责执行矩阵运算。Cube每次执行可完成两个float16类型的16x16矩阵的乘法操作。</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="7.23%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.3 " style="display:none"><p id="p34745063617"><a name="p34745063617"></a><a name="p34745063617"></a>CANN</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.43%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.4 " style="display:none"><p id="p37721610432"><a name="p37721610432"></a><a name="p37721610432"></a>牟楠</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="7.5200000000000005%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.5 " style="display:none"><p id="p9198162344220"><a name="p9198162344220"></a><a name="p9198162344220"></a>否</p>
<p id="p2198102312421"><a name="p2198102312421"></a><a name="p2198102312421"></a>需要修正术语库</p>

</tr>
<tr id="row1214817592317"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="14.14%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.1 "><p id="p12148175911319"><a name="p12148175911319"></a><a name="p12148175911319"></a><strong id="b1260272610461"><a name="b1260272610461"></a><a name="b1260272610461"></a>Container</strong></p>

<p id="p51485591835"><a name="p51485591835"></a><a name="p51485591835"></a>容器是一种操作系统虚拟化形式，可以使用一个容器来运行从小型微服务或软件进程到大型应用程序的所有内容。容器包含所有必要的可执行文件、二进制代码、库和配置文件。</p>
</td>

</tr>

</td>

</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="64.96%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.2 "><p id="p175916985610"><a name="p175916985610"></a><a name="p175916985610"></a>控制器，完成集群内所有<span id="ph3841644666"><a name="ph3841644666"></a><a name="ph3841644666"></a>Server</span>的业务状态管控、PD身份管理与决策、资源管理决策等，是整个集群的状态管控器和决策大脑。</p>
</td>

<tr id="row157947138569"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="14.14%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.1 "><p id="p5795101375617"><a name="p5795101375617"></a><a name="p5795101375617"></a><strong id="b3227198123517"><a name="b3227198123517"></a><a name="b3227198123517"></a>Coordinator</strong></p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="64.96%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.2 "><p id="p167951313155613"><a name="p167951313155613"></a><a name="p167951313155613"></a>调度器，是用户推理请求的入口，接收高并发的推理请求，进行请求调度、请求管理、请求转发等，是整个集群的数据请求入口。</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="7.23%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.3 " style="display:none"><p id="p1279591319562"><a name="p1279591319562"></a><a name="p1279591319562"></a>MindIE</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.43%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.4 " style="display:none"><p id="p1279551325614"><a name="p1279551325614"></a><a name="p1279551325614"></a>肖庆</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="7.5200000000000005%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.5 " style="display:none"><p id="p10795413145620"><a name="p10795413145620"></a><a name="p10795413145620"></a>否，待加入术语库</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row1534284165712"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="14.14%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.1 "><p id="p15342184113577"><a name="p15342184113577"></a><a name="p15342184113577"></a><strong id="b02401187352"><a name="b02401187352"></a><a name="b02401187352"></a>CP</strong></p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="64.96%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.2 "><p id="p11342114105718"><a name="p11342114105718"></a><a name="p11342114105718"></a>Context Parallelism，上下文并行。采用数据切分方式，将长输入序列按照cp_size拆分为多个子序列并行进行分块attention计算，分块计算后通过ring方式进行相邻rank的kv数据交换，实现长序列首token性能优化，适用于长序列输入场景下的P节点加速。</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="7.23%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.3 " style="display:none"><p id="p934264117574"><a name="p934264117574"></a><a name="p934264117574"></a>MindIE</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.43%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.4 " style="display:none"><p id="p1534284135715"><a name="p1534284135715"></a><a name="p1534284135715"></a>谢昌秀</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="7.5200000000000005%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.5 " style="display:none"><p id="p93421241185720"><a name="p93421241185720"></a><a name="p93421241185720"></a>否，待加入术语库</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row2626845115711"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="14.14%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.1 "><p id="p1262612454577"><a name="p1262612454577"></a><a name="p1262612454577"></a><strong id="b192414813515"><a name="b192414813515"></a><a name="b192414813515"></a>CertTools</strong></p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="64.96%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.2 "><p id="p2626184545717"><a name="p2626184545717"></a><a name="p2626184545717"></a>用于生成、配置、加密和管理 MindIE 服务化的证书与密钥，包括证书生成、证书/密钥导入及密钥加密等操作工具。</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="7.23%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.3 " style="display:none"><p id="p116271545115718"><a name="p116271545115718"></a><a name="p116271545115718"></a>MindIE</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.43%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.4 " style="display:none"><p id="p159961512115"><a name="p159961512115"></a><a name="p159961512115"></a>肖庆</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="7.5200000000000005%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.5 " style="display:none"><p id="p1662711451578"><a name="p1662711451578"></a><a name="p1662711451578"></a>是</p>
</td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>

## D<a name="section172041133920"></a>

<a name="table14972115318421"></a>

</th>
<th class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="64.96%" id="mcps1.1.6.1.2"><p id="p39861205439"><a name="p39861205439"></a><a name="p39861205439"></a><strong>含义</strong></p>
</th>
<th class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.529999999999999%" id="mcps1.1.6.1.3" style="display:none"><p id="p133261368610"><a name="p133261368610"></a><a name="p133261368610"></a>领域</p>
</th>
<th class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.8500000000000005%" id="mcps1.1.6.1.4" style="display:none"><p id="p33266361363"><a name="p33266361363"></a><a name="p33266361363"></a>负责人</p>
</th>
<th class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="7.5200000000000005%" id="mcps1.1.6.1.5" style="display:none"><p id="p11802184982013"><a name="p11802184982013"></a><a name="p11802184982013"></a>是否确认一致</p>
</th>
</tr>
</thead>
<tbody><tr id="row44451525155013"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="14.14%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.1 "><p id="p2559111311812"><a name="p2559111311812"></a><a name="p2559111311812"></a><strong id="b61401516131813"><a name="b61401516131813"></a><a name="b61401516131813"></a>Daemon</strong></p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="64.96%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.2 "><p id="p955911313182"><a name="p955911313182"></a><a name="p955911313182"></a>在 Linux/Unix 系统中，Daemon（守护进程）是一类脱离控制终端、在后台长期运行的系统服务进程，通常在系统启动时启动，并在系统关闭时退出。</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.529999999999999%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.3 " style="display:none"><p id="p5559191315182"><a name="p5559191315182"></a><a name="p5559191315182"></a>MindIE</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.8500000000000005%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.4 " style="display:none"><p id="p17560413191813"><a name="p17560413191813"></a><a name="p17560413191813"></a>肖庆</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="7.5200000000000005%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.5 " style="display:none"><p id="p208021249112019"><a name="p208021249112019"></a><a name="p208021249112019"></a>是</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row697265354216"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="14.14%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.1 "><p id="p14528185191613"><a name="p14528185191613"></a><a name="p14528185191613"></a><strong id="b98736565163"><a name="b98736565163"></a><a name="b98736565163"></a>DataFlow</strong></p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="64.96%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.2 "><p id="p1252818519169"><a name="p1252818519169"></a><a name="p1252818519169"></a>用于描述完整的计算流，该计算流由一个或多个计算处理点采用数据队列以数据驱动方式组成。</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.529999999999999%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.3 " style="display:none"><p id="p933111019373"><a name="p933111019373"></a><a name="p933111019373"></a>CANN</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.8500000000000005%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.4 " style="display:none"><p id="p106852815614"><a name="p106852815614"></a><a name="p106852815614"></a>刘晓芳</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="7.5200000000000005%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.5 " style="display:none"><p id="p4802124912011"><a name="p4802124912011"></a><a name="p4802124912011"></a>是</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row1479121920318"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="14.14%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.1 "><p id="p10689161216565"><a name="p10689161216565"></a><a name="p10689161216565"></a><strong id="b2097451975620"><a name="b2097451975620"></a><a name="b2097451975620"></a>DCMI</strong></p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="64.96%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.2 "><p id="zh-cn_topic_0000001206467210_zh-cn_topic_0000001223172951_zh-cn_topic_0000001173016448_p363132310287"><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001206467210_zh-cn_topic_0000001223172951_zh-cn_topic_0000001173016448_p363132310287"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001206467210_zh-cn_topic_0000001223172951_zh-cn_topic_0000001173016448_p363132310287"></a>Davinci Card Management Interface，设备管理接口。</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.529999999999999%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.3 " style="display:none"><p id="p1780773905712"><a name="p1780773905712"></a><a name="p1780773905712"></a>HDK</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.8500000000000005%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.4 " style="display:none"><p id="p15807103925717"><a name="p15807103925717"></a><a name="p15807103925717"></a>陈先雨</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="7.5200000000000005%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.5 " style="display:none"><p id="p14431164812204"><a name="p14431164812204"></a><a name="p14431164812204"></a>否</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row297285314214"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="14.14%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.1 "><p id="p43642448475"><a name="p43642448475"></a><a name="p43642448475"></a><strong id="b12184483473"><a name="b12184483473"></a><a name="b12184483473"></a>DDP</strong></p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="64.96%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.2 "><p id="p1545480619"><a name="p1545480619"></a><a name="p1545480619"></a>Distributed Data Parallel，分布式数据并行。</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.529999999999999%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.3 " style="display:none"><p id="p1133710133710"><a name="p1133710133710"></a><a name="p1133710133710"></a>CANN</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.8500000000000005%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.4 " style="display:none"><p id="p126811281368"><a name="p126811281368"></a><a name="p126811281368"></a>臧燕</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="7.5200000000000005%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.5 " style="display:none"><p id="p1180211499204"><a name="p1180211499204"></a><a name="p1180211499204"></a>是</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row197365316427"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="14.14%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.1 "><p id="p48172185"><a name="p48172185"></a><a name="p48172185"></a><strong id="b1259332434113"><a name="b1259332434113"></a><a name="b1259332434113"></a>DDR</strong></p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="64.96%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.2 "><p id="p9632925"><a name="p9632925"></a><a name="p9632925"></a>Double Data Rate，双倍数据速率。</p>
<p id="p1932091013253"><a name="p1932091013253"></a><a name="p1932091013253"></a>严格来说DDR应该叫DDR SDRAM，人们习惯称为DDR，其中，SDRAM是Synchronous Dynamic Random Access Memory的缩写，即同步动态随机存取存储器。而DDR SDRAM是Double Data Rate SDRAM的缩写，是双倍速率同步动态随机存储器的意思。DDR内存是在SDRAM内存基础上发展而来的，仍然沿用SDRAM生产体系，因此对于内存厂商而言，只需对制造普通SDRAM的设备稍加改进，即可实现DDR内存的生产，可有效的降低成本。</p>
<p id="p52180221"><a name="p52180221"></a><a name="p52180221"></a>与传统的单数据速率相比，DDR技术实现了一个时钟周期内进行两次读/写操作，即在时钟的上升沿和下降沿分别执行一次读/写操作。</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.529999999999999%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.3 " style="display:none"><p id="p9331110183710"><a name="p9331110183710"></a><a name="p9331110183710"></a>CANN</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.8500000000000005%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.4 " style="display:none"><p id="p1681287612"><a name="p1681287612"></a><a name="p1681287612"></a>叶成美</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="7.5200000000000005%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.5 " style="display:none"><p id="p280212492204"><a name="p280212492204"></a><a name="p280212492204"></a>是</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row1950533543417"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="14.14%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.1 "><p id="p2123105212419"><a name="p2123105212419"></a><a name="p2123105212419"></a><strong id="b13339612417"><a name="b13339612417"></a><a name="b13339612417"></a>DECS</strong></p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="64.96%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.2 "><p id="p12952311428"><a name="p12952311428"></a><a name="p12952311428"></a>Device-Edge-Cloud Synergy，端边云协同。</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.529999999999999%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.3 " style="display:none"><p id="p15658191533511"><a name="p15658191533511"></a><a name="p15658191533511"></a>Mind Edge</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.8500000000000005%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.4 " style="display:none"><p id="p18658121511357"><a name="p18658121511357"></a><a name="p18658121511357"></a>陆嘉怡</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="7.5200000000000005%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.5 " style="display:none"><p id="p380217495207"><a name="p380217495207"></a><a name="p380217495207"></a>是</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row497311535429"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="14.14%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.1 "><p id="p20618333"><a name="p20618333"></a><a name="p20618333"></a><strong id="b35941224134112"><a name="b35941224134112"></a><a name="b35941224134112"></a>DL</strong></p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="64.96%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.2 "><p id="p59472242"><a name="p59472242"></a><a name="p59472242"></a>Deep Learning，深度学习。</p>
<p id="p26445914"><a name="p26445914"></a><a name="p26445914"></a>是机器学习的分支，是一种试图使用包含复杂结构或由多重非线性变换构成的多个处理层对数据进行高层抽象的算法。</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.529999999999999%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.3 " style="display:none"><p id="p83319105374"><a name="p83319105374"></a><a name="p83319105374"></a>CANN</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.8500000000000005%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.4 " style="display:none"><p id="p4684287613"><a name="p4684287613"></a><a name="p4684287613"></a>叶成美</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="7.5200000000000005%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.5 " style="display:none"><p id="p8803124913206"><a name="p8803124913206"></a><a name="p8803124913206"></a>是</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row4289867118"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="14.14%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.1 "><p id="p209821459202418"><a name="p209821459202418"></a><a name="p209821459202418"></a><strong id="b77401429172520"><a name="b77401429172520"></a><a name="b77401429172520"></a>DMA</strong></p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="64.96%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.2 "><p id="p1998212595242"><a name="p1998212595242"></a><a name="p1998212595242"></a>Direct Memory Access，直接内存访问。</p>
<p id="p1939952811129"><a name="p1939952811129"></a><a name="p1939952811129"></a>是所有现代电脑的重要特色，它允许不同速度的硬件装置来沟通，而不需要依赖于CPU的大量中断负载。</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.529999999999999%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.3 " style="display:none"><p id="p87011916201312"><a name="p87011916201312"></a><a name="p87011916201312"></a><span>PTA</span></p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.8500000000000005%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.4 " style="display:none"><p id="p1270161671316"><a name="p1270161671316"></a><a name="p1270161671316"></a>刘倩</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="7.5200000000000005%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.5 " style="display:none"><p id="p1803749172010"><a name="p1803749172010"></a><a name="p1803749172010"></a>是</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row1498913336111"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="14.14%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.1 "><p id="p1598114662919"><a name="p1598114662919"></a><a name="p1598114662919"></a><strong id="b143911018153112"><a name="b143911018153112"></a><a name="b143911018153112"></a>DP</strong></p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="64.96%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.2 "><p id="p1110755183018"><a name="p1110755183018"></a><a name="p1110755183018"></a>Data Parallelism，数据并行。</p>
<p id="p203953211302"><a name="p203953211302"></a><a name="p203953211302"></a>数据并行是大规模深度学习训练中常用的并行模式，它会在每个进程（设备）中维护完整的模型和参数，但在每个进程上处理不同的数据。</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.529999999999999%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.3 " style="display:none"><p id="p373419541116"><a name="p373419541116"></a><a name="p373419541116"></a><span>PTA</span></p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.8500000000000005%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.4 " style="display:none"><p id="p10734155414110"><a name="p10734155414110"></a><a name="p10734155414110"></a>刘倩</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="7.5200000000000005%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.5 " style="display:none"><p id="p58030493202"><a name="p58030493202"></a><a name="p58030493202"></a>是</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row20995192871815"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="14.14%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.1 "><p id="p1248510362286"><a name="p1248510362286"></a><a name="p1248510362286"></a><strong id="b966932665615"><a name="b966932665615"></a><a name="b966932665615"></a>DPC</strong></p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="64.96%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.2 "><p id="p54852367288"><a name="p54852367288"></a><a name="p54852367288"></a>Distributed Parallel Client，分布式并行客户端。</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.529999999999999%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.3 " style="display:none"><p id="p6290194821814"><a name="p6290194821814"></a><a name="p6290194821814"></a>MindCluster集群调度</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.8500000000000005%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.4 " style="display:none"><p id="p1329017489182"><a name="p1329017489182"></a><a name="p1329017489182"></a>瞿铁铮</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="7.5200000000000005%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.5 " style="display:none"><p id="p1980364912019"><a name="p1980364912019"></a><a name="p1980364912019"></a>是</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row97091357202019"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="14.14%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.1 "><p id="p5990193005720"><a name="p5990193005720"></a><a name="p5990193005720"></a><strong id="b19880239155716"><a name="b19880239155716"></a><a name="b19880239155716"></a>DRAM</strong></p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="64.96%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.2 "><p id="p49901130155710"><a name="p49901130155710"></a><a name="p49901130155710"></a>Dynamic Random Access Memory，动态随机存储器。</p>
<p id="p181059141213"><a name="p181059141213"></a><a name="p181059141213"></a>DRAM是计算机主存储器的一种形式，用于临时存储CPU需要处理的数据和指令。</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.529999999999999%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.3 " style="display:none"><p id="p16881101192113"><a name="p16881101192113"></a><a name="p16881101192113"></a><span>PTA</span></p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.8500000000000005%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.4 " style="display:none"><p id="p088115182117"><a name="p088115182117"></a><a name="p088115182117"></a>刘倩</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="7.5200000000000005%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.5 " style="display:none"><p id="p178031649162011"><a name="p178031649162011"></a><a name="p178031649162011"></a>是</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row821185514316"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="14.14%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.1 "><p id="p724523113157"><a name="p724523113157"></a><a name="p724523113157"></a><strong id="b1255123317720"><a name="b1255123317720"></a><a name="b1255123317720"></a>DSL</strong></p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="64.96%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.2 "><p id="p02461031101511"><a name="p02461031101511"></a><a name="p02461031101511"></a>Domain Specific Language，领域专用语言。</p>
<p id="p8246163111159"><a name="p8246163111159"></a><a name="p8246163111159"></a>算子开发方式之一，用户仅需要使用DSL接口完成计算过程的表达，后续的算子调度、算子优化及编译都可通过已有的接口一键式完成。</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.529999999999999%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.3 " style="display:none"><p id="p155541530674"><a name="p155541530674"></a><a name="p155541530674"></a>MindStudio</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.8500000000000005%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.4 " style="display:none"><p id="p185544304719"><a name="p185544304719"></a><a name="p185544304719"></a>李翠</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="7.5200000000000005%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.5 " style="display:none"><p id="p94311748172019"><a name="p94311748172019"></a><a name="p94311748172019"></a>是，待上3ms术语库</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row19749535420"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="14.14%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.1 "><p id="p15170556154514"><a name="p15170556154514"></a><a name="p15170556154514"></a><strong id="b164361931144612"><a name="b164361931144612"></a><a name="b164361931144612"></a>DSCP</strong></p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="64.96%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.2 "><p id="p1517065610452"><a name="p1517065610452"></a><a name="p1517065610452"></a>Differentiated Services Code Point，区分服务编码点。</p>
<p id="p555166463"><a name="p555166463"></a><a name="p555166463"></a>根据Diff-Serv（Differentiated Service）的QoS分类标准，在每个数据包IP头部的服务类别TOS字节中，利用已使用的6比特和未使用的2比特，通过编码值来区分优先级。DSCP是TOS字节中已使用6比特的标识，是“IP 优先”和“服务类型”字段的组合。为了利用只支持“IP 优先”的旧路由器，会使用DSCP值，因为DSCP值与“IP 优先”字段兼容。每一个DSCP编码值都被映射到一个已定义的PHB（Per-Hop-Behavior）标识码。通过键入DSCP值，终端设备可对流量进行标识。</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.529999999999999%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.3 " style="display:none"><p id="p171709563459"><a name="p171709563459"></a><a name="p171709563459"></a>HDK</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.8500000000000005%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.4 " style="display:none"><p id="p617045624510"><a name="p617045624510"></a><a name="p617045624510"></a>陈先雨</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="7.5200000000000005%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.5 " style="display:none"><p id="p184313487201"><a name="p184313487201"></a><a name="p184313487201"></a>是</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row6975143818317"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="14.14%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.1 "><p id="p198451356155211"><a name="p198451356155211"></a><a name="p198451356155211"></a><strong id="b174450075314"><a name="b174450075314"></a><a name="b174450075314"></a>带宽</strong></p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="64.96%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.2 "><p id="p1842012825314"><a name="p1842012825314"></a><a name="p1842012825314"></a>网络中传输线路或通道能够承载的传输频率的范围。它实际上是传输线路或者通道上最高和最低频率之间的差。带宽越大，数据传输越快。</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.529999999999999%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.3 " style="display:none"><p id="p221541165312"><a name="p221541165312"></a><a name="p221541165312"></a>HDK</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.8500000000000005%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.4 " style="display:none"><p id="p18215111115537"><a name="p18215111115537"></a><a name="p18215111115537"></a>陈先雨</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="7.5200000000000005%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.5 " style="display:none"><p id="p3431184832010"><a name="p3431184832010"></a><a name="p3431184832010"></a>是</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row14974155364210"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="14.14%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.1 "><p id="p3685188171913"><a name="p3685188171913"></a><a name="p3685188171913"></a><strong id="b430625317511"><a name="b430625317511"></a><a name="b430625317511"></a>单算子比对</strong></p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="64.96%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.2 "><p id="p668516861916"><a name="p668516861916"></a><a name="p668516861916"></a>精度比对工具里Tensor比对的一种方式。选择网络模型中一个或多个参与计算的算子进行精度比对。</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.529999999999999%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.3 " style="display:none"><p id="p14331810103711"><a name="p14331810103711"></a><a name="p14331810103711"></a>CANN</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.8500000000000005%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.4 " style="display:none"><p id="p206862811615"><a name="p206862811615"></a><a name="p206862811615"></a>蔡伟伟</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="7.5200000000000005%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.5 " style="display:none"><p id="p1431114817206"><a name="p1431114817206"></a><a name="p1431114817206"></a>否，需要更新华为术语库</p>
</td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>

<p id="p60828573"><a name="p60828573"></a><a name="p60828573"></a>外部提供的接口和SD卡接口类似，内部存储介质为Flash，并且内建坏块管理系统。</p>

<p id="p2060542612465"><a name="p2060542612465"></a><a name="p2060542612465"></a>唯一标识设备的字符串，用以保证将License授权给指定设备的关键，又称“设备指纹”。</p>

</td>
</tr>

</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="64.96%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.2 "><p id="p8919131845918"><a name="p8919131845918"></a><a name="p8919131845918"></a>Expert Parallelism，专家并行。采用参数切分方式，将MoE（Mixture of Experts）模型中不同专家（experts）分配到不同设备，通过门控（gating）决定输入路由到哪些专家，来激活对应设备，适用于MoE架构类模型。</p>
</td>

</td>
</tr>

</table>

<tbody><tr id="row194261437135614"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="14.14%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.1 "><p id="p19184974535"><a name="p19184974535"></a><a name="p19184974535"></a><strong id="b613171010539"><a name="b613171010539"></a><a name="b613171010539"></a>Faiss</strong></p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="64.96%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.2 "><p id="p1218412715319"><a name="p1218412715319"></a><a name="p1218412715319"></a>Facebook开源的向量相似性搜索和聚类的库。</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.43%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.3 " style="display:none"><p id="p842683735619"><a name="p842683735619"></a><a name="p842683735619"></a>Mind SDK</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.950000000000001%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.4 " style="display:none"><p id="p1142633719564"><a name="p1142633719564"></a><a name="p1142633719564"></a>陆嘉怡</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="7.5200000000000005%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.5 " style="display:none"><p id="p11395132482211"><a name="p11395132482211"></a><a name="p11395132482211"></a>是</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row10870164413514"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="14.14%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.1 "><p id="p11471824125011"><a name="p11471824125011"></a><a name="p11471824125011"></a><strong id="b1262117266504"><a name="b1262117266504"></a><a name="b1262117266504"></a>FEC</strong></p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="64.96%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.2 "><p id="p17851618165012"><a name="p17851618165012"></a><a name="p17851618165012"></a>Forward Error Correction，前向纠错编码。</p>
<p id="p15412037105013"><a name="p15412037105013"></a><a name="p15412037105013"></a>一种通信技术，在数据传输过程中通过添加冗余数据来纠正传输错误。</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.43%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.3 " style="display:none"><p id="p18786718175018"><a name="p18786718175018"></a><a name="p18786718175018"></a>HDK</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.950000000000001%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.4 " style="display:none"><p id="p5786918125013"><a name="p5786918125013"></a><a name="p5786918125013"></a>陈先雨</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="7.5200000000000005%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.5 " style="display:none"><p id="p53959248228"><a name="p53959248228"></a><a name="p53959248228"></a>是</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row99041012103212"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="14.14%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.1 "><p id="p11904171219322"><a name="p11904171219322"></a><a name="p11904171219322"></a><strong id="b1398814176322"><a name="b1398814176322"></a><a name="b1398814176322"></a>FFT</strong></p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="64.96%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.2 "><p id="p654685262816"><a name="p654685262816"></a><a name="p654685262816"></a>Fast Fourier Transform，快速傅里叶变换。</p>
<p id="p195367357233"><a name="p195367357233"></a><a name="p195367357233"></a>快速傅里叶变换（FFT）是一种计算序列的离散傅立叶变换（DFT）或其逆变换（IDFT）的算法。傅里叶分析将信号从其原始域（通常是时间或空间）转换为频域中的表示，反之亦然。</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.43%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.3 " style="display:none"><p id="p1990414122321"><a name="p1990414122321"></a><a name="p1990414122321"></a>CANN信号加速库</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.950000000000001%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.4 " style="display:none"><p id="p1290471216324"><a name="p1290471216324"></a><a name="p1290471216324"></a>冯英</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="7.5200000000000005%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.5 " style="display:none"><p id="p590414124324"><a name="p590414124324"></a><a name="p590414124324"></a>是</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row383523516514"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="14.14%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.1 "><p id="p2260142412016"><a name="p2260142412016"></a><a name="p2260142412016"></a><strong id="b982923112010"><a name="b982923112010"></a><a name="b982923112010"></a>FFTS</strong></p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="64.96%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.2 "><p id="p526022422016"><a name="p526022422016"></a><a name="p526022422016"></a>Function Flow Task Scheduler，函数流任务调度。</p>
<p id="p1187102364016"><a name="p1187102364016"></a><a name="p1187102364016"></a>这是一种全新的基于数据流驱动的并行调度机制。它提供了子图DMU机制，去除子图内不必要的DMA拷贝开销，同时提供了sub task的thread切分和thread间并行调度机制，以尽可能并行AIC/AIV硬件资源，达到算子间融合效果。</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.43%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.3 " style="display:none"><p id="p163741232132418"><a name="p163741232132418"></a><a name="p163741232132418"></a><span>PTA</span></p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.950000000000001%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.4 " style="display:none"><p id="p18374183252412"><a name="p18374183252412"></a><a name="p18374183252412"></a>刘倩</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="7.5200000000000005%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.5 " style="display:none"><p id="p1439532482212"><a name="p1439532482212"></a><a name="p1439532482212"></a>是</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row158122311514"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="14.14%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.1 "><p id="p4641237125019"><a name="p4641237125019"></a><a name="p4641237125019"></a><strong id="b15959114745120"><a name="b15959114745120"></a><a name="b15959114745120"></a>Flash Attention</strong></p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="64.96%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.2 "><p id="p1836416331432"><a name="p1836416331432"></a><a name="p1836416331432"></a>Flash Attention融合算子是一种应用于模型加速的具有IO感知的精确注意力算法，它无需任何近似即可加速注意力计算并减少内存占用。</p>
<p id="p1764123710502"><a name="p1764123710502"></a><a name="p1764123710502"></a>Flash Attention融合算子在大语言模型中广泛使用，例如LLaMA、GPT3等。</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.43%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.3 " style="display:none"><p id="p164884792318"><a name="p164884792318"></a><a name="p164884792318"></a><span>PTA</span></p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.950000000000001%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.4 " style="display:none"><p id="p34891372239"><a name="p34891372239"></a><a name="p34891372239"></a>刘倩</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="7.5200000000000005%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.5 " style="display:none"><p id="p4395152482219"><a name="p4395152482219"></a><a name="p4395152482219"></a>否，待加入术语库</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row14896123371120"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="14.14%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.1 "><p id="p6755232"><a name="p6755232"></a><a name="p6755232"></a><strong id="b5199355104612"><a name="b5199355104612"></a><a name="b5199355104612"></a>FLOPS</strong></p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="64.96%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.2 "><p id="p10302948"><a name="p10302948"></a><a name="p10302948"></a>Floating-Point Operations Per Second，每秒所执行的浮点运算次数。</p>
<p id="p19019073"><a name="p19019073"></a><a name="p19019073"></a>常被用来估算电脑的执行效能，尤其是在使用到大量浮点运算的科学计算领域中。正因为FLOPS字尾的那个S，代表秒，而不是复数，所以不能省略掉。</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.43%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.3 " style="display:none"><p id="p2035132816376"><a name="p2035132816376"></a><a name="p2035132816376"></a>CANN</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.950000000000001%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.4 " style="display:none"><p id="p6583125391115"><a name="p6583125391115"></a><a name="p6583125391115"></a>陈萍萍</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="7.5200000000000005%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.5 " style="display:none"><p id="p139562414229"><a name="p139562414229"></a><a name="p139562414229"></a>是</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row33371055258"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="14.14%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.1 "><p id="p4511154715174"><a name="p4511154715174"></a><a name="p4511154715174"></a><strong id="b12209058810"><a name="b12209058810"></a><a name="b12209058810"></a>FP Point</strong></p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="64.96%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.2 "><p id="p851114716178"><a name="p851114716178"></a><a name="p851114716178"></a>Forward Propagation Point，训练网络迭代轨迹正向算子的开始位置。</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.43%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.3 " style="display:none"><p id="p598951081"><a name="p598951081"></a><a name="p598951081"></a>MindStudio</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.950000000000001%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.4 " style="display:none"><p id="p8989181382"><a name="p8989181382"></a><a name="p8989181382"></a>蔡伟伟</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="7.5200000000000005%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.5 " style="display:none"><p id="p2395824182217"><a name="p2395824182217"></a><a name="p2395824182217"></a>是</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row118010573569"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="14.14%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.1 "><p id="p354893275412"><a name="p354893275412"></a><a name="p354893275412"></a><strong id="b11067147221"><a name="b11067147221"></a><a name="b11067147221"></a>FUSE</strong></p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="64.96%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.2 "><p id="p16711191619395"><a name="p16711191619395"></a><a name="p16711191619395"></a>Filesystem in Userspace，用户空间文件系统。</p>
<p id="p1754718325547"><a name="p1754718325547"></a><a name="p1754718325547"></a>FUSE是操作系统中的概念，指完全在用户态实现的文件系统。目前Linux通过内核模块对此进行支持。一些文件系统如ZFS，glusterfs和lustre使用FUSE实现。</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.43%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.3 " style="display:none"><p id="p1981175717567"><a name="p1981175717567"></a><a name="p1981175717567"></a>Mind SDK</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.950000000000001%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.4 " style="display:none"><p id="p2811057145613"><a name="p2811057145613"></a><a name="p2811057145613"></a>陆嘉怡</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="7.5200000000000005%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.5 " style="display:none"><p id="p163953243223"><a name="p163953243223"></a><a name="p163953243223"></a>是</p>
</td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>

## G<a name="section1843341915559"></a>

<a name="table1194125818555"></a>
<table><thead align="left"><tr id="row199418583552"><th class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="14.14%" id="mcps1.1.6.1.1"><p id="p1542618219516"><a name="p1542618219516"></a><a name="p1542618219516"></a><strong>术语/缩略语</strong></p>
</th>
<th class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="64.96%" id="mcps1.1.6.1.2"><p id="p134269211158"><a name="p134269211158"></a><a name="p134269211158"></a><strong>含义</strong></p>
</th>
<th class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.43%" id="mcps1.1.6.1.3" style="display:none"><p id="p1142711211250"><a name="p1142711211250"></a><a name="p1142711211250"></a>领域</p>
</th>
<th class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.950000000000001%" id="mcps1.1.6.1.4" style="display:none"><p id="p7427162110515"><a name="p7427162110515"></a><a name="p7427162110515"></a>负责人</p>
</th>
<th class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="7.5200000000000005%" id="mcps1.1.6.1.5" style="display:none"><p id="p0448443234"><a name="p0448443234"></a><a name="p0448443234"></a>是否确认一致</p>
</th>
</tr>
</thead>
<tbody><tr id="row0941258155518"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="14.14%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.1 "><p id="p339614974618"><a name="p339614974618"></a><a name="p339614974618"></a><strong id="b1455814562461"><a name="b1455814562461"></a><a name="b1455814562461"></a>GDAT</strong></p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="64.96%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.2 "><p id="p439624912468"><a name="p439624912468"></a><a name="p439624912468"></a>Gradient Auto Tuning，梯度自动调优。</p>
<p id="p426841413476"><a name="p426841413476"></a><a name="p426841413476"></a>GDAT是通过最大化反向计算与梯度聚合通信并行度，缩短通信拖尾时间的优化工具。分布式训练场景下，各个设备之间计算梯度后执行梯度聚合操作，梯度聚合算子的融合策略会影响反向计算结束后的通信拖尾时间，从而影响集群训练的性能和线性度。</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.43%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.3 " style="display:none"><p id="p18444173111379"><a name="p18444173111379"></a><a name="p18444173111379"></a>CANN</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.950000000000001%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.4 " style="display:none"><p id="p188651295512"><a name="p188651295512"></a><a name="p188651295512"></a>刘晓芳</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="7.5200000000000005%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.5 " style="display:none"><p id="p144816432318"><a name="p144816432318"></a><a name="p144816432318"></a>是</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row58671659121814"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="14.14%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.1 "><p id="p06391698532"><a name="p06391698532"></a><a name="p06391698532"></a><strong id="b963909155315"><a name="b963909155315"></a><a name="b963909155315"></a>GDB</strong></p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="64.96%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.2 "><p id="p263909115314"><a name="p263909115314"></a><a name="p263909115314"></a>GNU Debugger，GNU工程调试器。可以监控其它程序运行时的内部情况，或者其它程序要崩溃时发生了什么。GDB支持如下四种主要操作，辅助查找缺陷。</p>
<a name="ul51901142153620"></a><a name="ul51901142153620"></a><ul id="ul51901142153620"><li>启动程序，指定可能影响行为的任何因素。</li><li>特定条件下，停止程序。</li><li>程序停止时，检查发生了什么。</li><li>修改程序内容，尝试纠正一个缺陷并继续下一个。</li></ul>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.43%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.3 " style="display:none"><p id="p5868155951813"><a name="p5868155951813"></a><a name="p5868155951813"></a>MindSDK</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.950000000000001%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.4 " style="display:none"><p id="p786812598189"><a name="p786812598189"></a><a name="p786812598189"></a>陆嘉怡</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="7.5200000000000005%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.5 " style="display:none"><p id="p91377610379"><a name="p91377610379"></a><a name="p91377610379"></a>是</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row16941158195515"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="14.14%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.1 "><p id="p985711695617"><a name="p985711695617"></a><a name="p985711695617"></a><strong id="b046815720469"><a name="b046815720469"></a><a name="b046815720469"></a>GE</strong></p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="64.96%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.2 "><p id="p2857716165610"><a name="p2857716165610"></a><a name="p2857716165610"></a>Graph Engine，图引擎。</p>
<p id="p10857151612563"><a name="p10857151612563"></a><a name="p10857151612563"></a>提供了Graph/Operator IR作为安全易用的构图接口集合，用户可以调用这些接口构建网络模型，设置模型所包含的图、图内的算子、以及模型和算子的属性。</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.43%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.3 " style="display:none"><p id="p14444123119373"><a name="p14444123119373"></a><a name="p14444123119373"></a>CANN</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.950000000000001%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.4 " style="display:none"><p id="p11865591516"><a name="p11865591516"></a><a name="p11865591516"></a>陈萍萍</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="7.5200000000000005%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.5 " style="display:none"><p id="p84488432315"><a name="p84488432315"></a><a name="p84488432315"></a>否</p>
<p id="p6390652195314"><a name="p6390652195314"></a><a name="p6390652195314"></a>未上3ms，待录入</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row99514580557"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="14.14%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.1 "><p id="p11755143601315"><a name="p11755143601315"></a><a name="p11755143601315"></a><strong id="b763923914135"><a name="b763923914135"></a><a name="b763923914135"></a>Global Memory/GM</strong></p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="64.96%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.2 "><p id="p77551836141320"><a name="p77551836141320"></a><a name="p77551836141320"></a>设备端的主内存，AI Core的外部存储，用于存储大规模数据，但需要优化访问模式以提升性能。</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.43%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.3 " style="display:none"><p id="p5774432173714"><a name="p5774432173714"></a><a name="p5774432173714"></a>CANN</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.950000000000001%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.4 " style="display:none"><p id="p10865189851"><a name="p10865189851"></a><a name="p10865189851"></a>牟楠</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="7.5200000000000005%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.5 " style="display:none"><p id="p112215335489"><a name="p112215335489"></a><a name="p112215335489"></a>否</p>
<p id="p1112213339482"><a name="p1112213339482"></a><a name="p1112213339482"></a>需要修正术语库</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row13878846181911"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="14.14%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.1 "><p id="p1879946111910"><a name="p1879946111910"></a><a name="p1879946111910"></a><strong id="b1661344991917"><a name="b1661344991917"></a><a name="b1661344991917"></a>gRPC</strong></p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="64.96%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.2 "><p id="p2087917464195"><a name="p2087917464195"></a><a name="p2087917464195"></a>Google Remote Procedure Call，Google远程过程调用协议。</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.43%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.3 " style="display:none"><p id="p12231159121914"><a name="p12231159121914"></a><a name="p12231159121914"></a>MindIE</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.950000000000001%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.4 " style="display:none"><p id="p723165911916"><a name="p723165911916"></a><a name="p723165911916"></a>肖庆</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="7.5200000000000005%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.5 " style="display:none"><p id="p24484412232"><a name="p24484412232"></a><a name="p24484412232"></a>是</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row12252611571"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="14.14%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.1 "><p id="p0232026165716"><a name="p0232026165716"></a><a name="p0232026165716"></a><strong id="b163628356572"><a name="b163628356572"></a><a name="b163628356572"></a>GRPO</strong></p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="64.96%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.2 "><p id="p82392610573"><a name="p82392610573"></a><a name="p82392610573"></a>Group Relative Policy Optimization，群体相对策略优化。</p>
<p id="p1175492135819"><a name="p1175492135819"></a><a name="p1175492135819"></a>GRPO是一种强化学习算法，专门用于增强大型语言模型中的推理能力。与严重依赖外部评估模型（价值函数）指导学习的传统强化学习方法不同，GRPO通过评估彼此相关的响应组来优化模型，以此提高训练效率。</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.43%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.3 " style="display:none"><p id="p1205041844"><a name="p1205041844"></a><a name="p1205041844"></a><span>PTA</span></p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.950000000000001%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.4 " style="display:none"><p id="p52051411147"><a name="p52051411147"></a><a name="p52051411147"></a>刘倩</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="7.5200000000000005%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.5 " style="display:none"><p id="p12059411418"><a name="p12059411418"></a><a name="p12059411418"></a>是</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row8649434207"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="14.14%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.1 "><p id="p66584315203"><a name="p66584315203"></a><a name="p66584315203"></a><strong id="b2128172664718"><a name="b2128172664718"></a><a name="b2128172664718"></a>管理面</strong></p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="64.96%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.2 "><p id="p1565184312204"><a name="p1565184312204"></a><a name="p1565184312204"></a>健康状态信息接口所处的平面。</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.43%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.3 " style="display:none"><p id="p10535059162015"><a name="p10535059162015"></a><a name="p10535059162015"></a>MindIE</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.950000000000001%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.4 " style="display:none"><p id="p5535559132010"><a name="p5535559132010"></a><a name="p5535559132010"></a>肖庆</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="7.5200000000000005%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.5 " style="display:none"><p id="p134483415232"><a name="p134483415232"></a><a name="p134483415232"></a>否，待加入术语库</p>
</td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>

<p id="p1341017526912"><a name="p1341017526912"></a><a name="p1341017526912"></a>摄影术语，用来描述媒体应用，如数字影像和数字音频。</p>

<p id="p1894653283512"><a name="p1894653283512"></a><a name="p1894653283512"></a>在Kubernetes中，Horizontal Pod Autoscaler自动更新工作负载资源（例如Deployment或者StatefulSet），目的是自动扩缩工作负载以满足需求。</p>
</td>

</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row922312491797"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="14.14%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.1 "><p id="p84150522098"><a name="p84150522098"></a><a name="p84150522098"></a><strong id="b1141514521097"><a name="b1141514521097"></a><a name="b1141514521097"></a>HPO</strong></p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="64.96%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.2 "><p id="p1641514520913"><a name="p1641514520913"></a><a name="p1641514520913"></a>Hyperparameter Optimization，超参数优化。</p>
<p id="p164153521095"><a name="p164153521095"></a><a name="p164153521095"></a><span>是指不是依赖人工调整参数，而是通过一定算法找出机器学习或深度学习中最优/次优超参数的一类方法</span><span>。</span></p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.43%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.3 " style="display:none"><p id="p1741535217914"><a name="p1741535217914"></a><a name="p1741535217914"></a>PTA</p>

</td>
</tr>

<a name="table19239204171111"></a>
