</th>
<th class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.43%" id="mcps1.1.6.1.3" style="display:none"><p id="p102592012267"><a name="p102592012267"></a><a name="p102592012267"></a>领域</p>
</th>
<th class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.950000000000001%" id="mcps1.1.6.1.4" style="display:none"><p id="p625102072613"><a name="p625102072613"></a><a name="p625102072613"></a>负责人</p>
</th>
<th class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="7.5200000000000005%" id="mcps1.1.6.1.5" style="display:none"><p id="p873717286264"><a name="p873717286264"></a><a name="p873717286264"></a>是否确认一致</p>
</th>
</tr>
</thead>
<tbody><tr id="row186321520114812"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="14.14%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.1 "><p id="p1483574075617"><a name="p1483574075617"></a><a name="p1483574075617"></a><strong id="b1083524012565"><a name="b1083524012565"></a><a name="b1083524012565"></a>KMC</strong></p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="64.96%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.2 "><p id="p1983534018561"><a name="p1983534018561"></a><a name="p1983534018561"></a>Key Management CBB，密钥管理组件。</p>
<p id="p148351740105613"><a name="p148351740105613"></a><a name="p148351740105613"></a>为实现密钥管理的代码共享、简化开发而设计的密钥管理模块，该模块实现了密钥的加密保存、加密解密等基本功能，方便产品快速集成密钥功能。</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.43%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.3 " style="display:none"><p id="p019561611227"><a name="p019561611227"></a><a name="p019561611227"></a>MindCluster集群调度</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.950000000000001%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.4 " style="display:none"><p id="p101953163228"><a name="p101953163228"></a><a name="p101953163228"></a>瞿铁铮</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="7.5200000000000005%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.5 " style="display:none"><p id="p1573792852615"><a name="p1573792852615"></a><a name="p1573792852615"></a>是</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row1787412240486"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="14.14%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.1 "><p id="p168561076196"><a name="p168561076196"></a><a name="p168561076196"></a><strong id="b34951411131910"><a name="b34951411131910"></a><a name="b34951411131910"></a>KMC</strong></p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="64.96%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.2 "><p id="p227565206"><a name="p227565206"></a><a name="p227565206"></a>Key Management Center，密钥管理系统。</p>
<p id="p168563791920"><a name="p168563791920"></a><a name="p168563791920"></a>KMC用于管理和保护加密算法中使用的密钥。它可以为企业或组织提供安全的密钥存储、密钥分发、密钥轮换、密钥备份和密钥恢复等功能。KMC密钥库可以确保密钥的安全性和可靠性，防止密钥泄露、丢失或被篡改。同时，KMC密钥库还可以支持多种加密算法和密钥长度，满足不同应用场景的需求。</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.43%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.3 " style="display:none"><p id="p14856873194"><a name="p14856873194"></a><a name="p14856873194"></a>MindIE</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.950000000000001%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.4 " style="display:none"><p id="p88562716196"><a name="p88562716196"></a><a name="p88562716196"></a>肖庆</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="7.5200000000000005%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.5 " style="display:none"><p id="p2737192810262"><a name="p2737192810262"></a><a name="p2737192810262"></a>否，待加入术语库</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row1487445293511"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="14.14%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.1 "><p id="p74049381218"><a name="p74049381218"></a><a name="p74049381218"></a><strong id="b732814115241"><a name="b732814115241"></a><a name="b732814115241"></a>Kullback-Leibler Divergence</strong></p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="64.96%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.2 "><p id="p1097650172619"><a name="p1097650172619"></a><a name="p1097650172619"></a>KL散度算法。</p>
<p id="p114041038126"><a name="p114041038126"></a><a name="p114041038126"></a>精度比对算法之一，计算结果取值范围为0到无穷大。KL散度越小，真实分布与近似分布之间的匹配越好。</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.43%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.3 " style="display:none"><p id="p205419105385"><a name="p205419105385"></a><a name="p205419105385"></a>MindStudio</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.950000000000001%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.4 " style="display:none"><p id="p63445156262"><a name="p63445156262"></a><a name="p63445156262"></a>蔡伟伟</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="7.5200000000000005%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.5 " style="display:none"><p id="p193751719493"><a name="p193751719493"></a><a name="p193751719493"></a>否，待加入术语库</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row1753111476225"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="14.14%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.1 "><p id="p8836240105617"><a name="p8836240105617"></a><a name="p8836240105617"></a><strong id="b17836114011563"><a name="b17836114011563"></a><a name="b17836114011563"></a>Kubernetes</strong></p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="64.96%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.2 "><p id="p11836540115618"><a name="p11836540115618"></a><a name="p11836540115618"></a>Kubernetes是用于自动部署、扩展和管理容器化（containerized）应用程序的开源系统。它旨在提供“跨主机集群的自动部署、扩展以及运行应用程序容器的平台”。</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.43%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.3 " style="display:none"><p id="p5531194712215"><a name="p5531194712215"></a><a name="p5531194712215"></a>MindCluster集群调度</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.950000000000001%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.4 " style="display:none"><p id="p553111475229"><a name="p553111475229"></a><a name="p553111475229"></a>瞿铁铮</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="7.5200000000000005%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.5 " style="display:none"><p id="p27379281264"><a name="p27379281264"></a><a name="p27379281264"></a>是</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row168671321312"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="14.14%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.1 "><p id="p5867203211318"><a name="p5867203211318"></a><a name="p5867203211318"></a><strong id="b1179119552359"><a name="b1179119552359"></a><a name="b1179119552359"></a>KASLR</strong></p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="64.96%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.2 "><p id="p1086743216318"><a name="p1086743216318"></a><a name="p1086743216318"></a>Kernel Address Space Layout Randomization是一种安全机制，开启后可以增加针对内核漏洞的攻击难度。</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.43%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.3 " style="display:none"><p id="p12867183218310"><a name="p12867183218310"></a><a name="p12867183218310"></a>MindIE</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.950000000000001%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.4 " style="display:none"><p id="p1986703219315"><a name="p1986703219315"></a><a name="p1986703219315"></a>谢昌秀</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="7.5200000000000005%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.5 " style="display:none"><p id="p108672032834"><a name="p108672032834"></a><a name="p108672032834"></a>否，需要更新华为术语库</p>
</td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>

<th class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.94%" id="mcps1.1.6.1.4" style="display:none"><p id="p18993125110380"><a name="p18993125110380"></a><a name="p18993125110380"></a>负责人</p>

<th class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="7.53%" id="mcps1.1.6.1.5" style="display:none"><p id="p114471083269"><a name="p114471083269"></a><a name="p114471083269"></a>是否确认一致</p>
</th>
</tr>
</thead>
<tbody><tr id="row124411740163813"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="14.14%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.1 "><p id="p11931713141012"><a name="p11931713141012"></a><a name="p11931713141012"></a><strong id="b5522181631015"><a name="b5522181631015"></a><a name="b5522181631015"></a>L0A Buffer</strong></p>
</td>

</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.43%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.3 " style="display:none"><p id="p754211013389"><a name="p754211013389"></a><a name="p754211013389"></a>CANN</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.94%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.4 " style="display:none"><p id="p2345201512612"><a name="p2345201512612"></a><a name="p2345201512612"></a>牟楠</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="7.53%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.5 " style="display:none"><p id="p1144711816265"><a name="p1144711816265"></a><a name="p1144711816265"></a>否，待加入术语库</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row2044264010387"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="14.14%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.1 "><p id="p1993131314102"><a name="p1993131314102"></a><a name="p1993131314102"></a><strong id="b752361612105"><a name="b752361612105"></a><a name="b752361612105"></a>L0B Buffer</strong></p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="64.96%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.2 "><p id="p18364328105110"><a name="p18364328105110"></a><a name="p18364328105110"></a>AI Core内部物理存储单元，通常用于存储矩阵计算的右矩阵，与逻辑内存AscendC::TPosition::B2相对应。</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.43%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.3 " style="display:none"><p id="p15542110103813"><a name="p15542110103813"></a><a name="p15542110103813"></a>CANN</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.94%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.4 " style="display:none"><p id="p1334515157266"><a name="p1334515157266"></a><a name="p1334515157266"></a>牟楠</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="7.53%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.5 " style="display:none"><p id="p1944748172619"><a name="p1944748172619"></a><a name="p1944748172619"></a>否，待加入术语库</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row044244015385"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="14.14%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.1 "><p id="p293131331010"><a name="p293131331010"></a><a name="p293131331010"></a><strong id="b12524716111014"><a name="b12524716111014"></a><a name="b12524716111014"></a>L0C Buffer</strong></p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="64.96%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.2 "><p id="p83644289519"><a name="p83644289519"></a><a name="p83644289519"></a>AI Core内部物理存储单元，通常用于存储矩阵计算的结果，与逻辑内存AscendC::TPosition::CO1相对应。</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.43%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.3 " style="display:none"><p id="p1354251018384"><a name="p1354251018384"></a><a name="p1354251018384"></a>CANN</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.94%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.4 " style="display:none"><p id="p123453151266"><a name="p123453151266"></a><a name="p123453151266"></a>牟楠</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="7.53%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.5 " style="display:none"><p id="p1844710842617"><a name="p1844710842617"></a><a name="p1844710842617"></a>否，待加入术语库</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row244264018386"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="14.14%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.1 "><p id="p037752971015"><a name="p037752971015"></a><a name="p037752971015"></a><strong id="b1263493110107"><a name="b1263493110107"></a><a name="b1263493110107"></a>L1 Buffer</strong></p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="64.96%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.2 "><p id="p1836472812518"><a name="p1836472812518"></a><a name="p1836472812518"></a>AI Core内部物理存储单元，空间相对较大，通常用于缓存矩阵计算的输入数据。矩阵计算的输入一般需要从GM搬运到L1 Buffer，然后分别搬运到L0A Buffer和L0B Buffer。L1 Buffer与逻辑内存AscendC::TPosition::A1、AscendC::TPosition::B1相对应。</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.43%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.3 " style="display:none"><p id="p17542151023817"><a name="p17542151023817"></a><a name="p17542151023817"></a>CANN</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.94%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.4 " style="display:none"><p id="p1034516150268"><a name="p1034516150268"></a><a name="p1034516150268"></a>牟楠</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="7.53%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.5 " style="display:none"><p id="p1344713820261"><a name="p1344713820261"></a><a name="p1344713820261"></a>否，待加入术语库</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row41481540124217"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="14.14%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.1 "><p id="p73581320151913"><a name="p73581320151913"></a><a name="p73581320151913"></a><strong id="b107856910106"><a name="b107856910106"></a><a name="b107856910106"></a>L2 Cache</strong></p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="64.96%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.2 "><p id="p83581320101918"><a name="p83581320101918"></a><a name="p83581320101918"></a>Level 2 Cache，二级缓存。</p>
<p id="p173581020141910"><a name="p173581020141910"></a><a name="p173581020141910"></a>L2 Cache 是 CPU 的第二级缓存，用于在访问主存之前更快地提供常用数据和指令。</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.43%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.3 " style="display:none"><p id="p10415153121020"><a name="p10415153121020"></a><a name="p10415153121020"></a>MindStudio</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.94%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.4 " style="display:none"><p id="p241613315101"><a name="p241613315101"></a><a name="p241613315101"></a>李翠</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="7.53%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.5 " style="display:none"><p id="p19447178122618"><a name="p19447178122618"></a><a name="p19447178122618"></a>是，待上3ms术语库</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row1118155144212"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="14.14%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.1 "><p id="p1257812112711"><a name="p1257812112711"></a><a name="p1257812112711"></a><strong id="b288002017270"><a name="b288002017270"></a><a name="b288002017270"></a>LLDP</strong></p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="64.96%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.2 "><p id="p4257171232719"><a name="p4257171232719"></a><a name="p4257171232719"></a>Link Layer Discovery Protocol，链路层发现协议。</p>
<p id="p674574918286"><a name="p674574918286"></a><a name="p674574918286"></a>链路层发现协议是IEEE 802.1ab中定义的第二层发现协议。通过采用LLDP技术，在网络规模迅速扩大时，网管系统可以快速掌握二层网络拓扑信息、拓扑变化信息。</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.43%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.3 " style="display:none"><p id="p3257101212718"><a name="p3257101212718"></a><a name="p3257101212718"></a>HDK</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.94%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.4 " style="display:none"><p id="p725751232714"><a name="p725751232714"></a><a name="p725751232714"></a>陈先雨</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="7.53%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.5 " style="display:none"><p id="p2447208172615"><a name="p2447208172615"></a><a name="p2447208172615"></a>是</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row1944410404382"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="14.14%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.1 "><p id="p1562620499396"><a name="p1562620499396"></a><a name="p1562620499396"></a><strong id="b19541956113918"><a name="b19541956113918"></a><a name="b19541956113918"></a>LLM</strong></p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="64.96%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.2 "><p id="p56261749183913"><a name="p56261749183913"></a><a name="p56261749183913"></a><span>Large Language Model，大语言模型。</span></p>
<p id="p23545592595"><a name="p23545592595"></a><a name="p23545592595"></a>大语言模型是一种语言模型，由具有许多参数（通常数十亿个权重或更多）的人工神经网络组成，使用自监督学习或半监督学习对大量未标记文本进行训练。</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.43%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.3 " style="display:none"><p id="p1654210107385"><a name="p1654210107385"></a><a name="p1654210107385"></a>CANN</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.94%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.4 " style="display:none"><p id="p13451151268"><a name="p13451151268"></a><a name="p13451151268"></a>刘晓芳</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="7.53%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.5 " style="display:none"><p id="p1744716814267"><a name="p1744716814267"></a><a name="p1744716814267"></a>是</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row2044494083818"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="14.14%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.1 "><p id="p1067515586123"><a name="p1067515586123"></a><a name="p1067515586123"></a><strong id="b7424412201317"><a name="b7424412201317"></a><a name="b7424412201317"></a>Local Memory</strong></p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="64.96%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.2 "><p id="p9675158161215"><a name="p9675158161215"></a><a name="p9675158161215"></a>AI Core的内部存储，包括L1 Buffer、L0A Buffer、L0B Buffer、L0C Buffer、Unified Buffer等存储单元。</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.43%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.3 " style="display:none"><p id="p20542151093814"><a name="p20542151093814"></a><a name="p20542151093814"></a>CANN</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.94%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.4 " style="display:none"><p id="p133458155268"><a name="p133458155268"></a><a name="p133458155268"></a>牟楠</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="7.53%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.5 " style="display:none"><p id="p1447188112610"><a name="p1447188112610"></a><a name="p1447188112610"></a>否，待加入术语库</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row18444740203815"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="14.14%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.1 "><p id="p8534582352"><a name="p8534582352"></a><a name="p8534582352"></a><strong id="b3561111218359"><a name="b3561111218359"></a><a name="b3561111218359"></a>Loss</strong></p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="64.96%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.2 "><p id="p653468113512"><a name="p653468113512"></a><a name="p653468113512"></a>损失，预测值与实际值的偏差，深度学习用于判断模型好坏的一个标准。</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.43%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.3 " style="display:none"><p id="p55427100383"><a name="p55427100383"></a><a name="p55427100383"></a>CANN</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.94%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.4 " style="display:none"><p id="p73451915102615"><a name="p73451915102615"></a><a name="p73451915102615"></a>臧燕/陈萍</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="7.53%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.5 " style="display:none"><p id="p1158419451909"><a name="p1158419451909"></a><a name="p1158419451909"></a>是</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row665118321389"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="14.14%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.1 "><p id="p9935901219"><a name="p9935901219"></a><a name="p9935901219"></a><strong id="b88593517131"><a name="b88593517131"></a><a name="b88593517131"></a>LTO</strong></p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="64.96%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.2 "><p id="p139145931216"><a name="p139145931216"></a><a name="p139145931216"></a>Link Time Optimization，链接时优化。</p>
<p id="p976612162167"><a name="p976612162167"></a><a name="p976612162167"></a>链接时优化是编译器在链接时对程序进行的一种程序优化。</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.43%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.3 " style="display:none"><p id="p1799818421985"><a name="p1799818421985"></a><a name="p1799818421985"></a><span>PTA</span></p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.94%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.4 " style="display:none"><p id="p1799810421387"><a name="p1799810421387"></a><a name="p1799810421387"></a>刘倩</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="7.53%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.5 " style="display:none"><p id="p544710802620"><a name="p544710802620"></a><a name="p544710802620"></a>是</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row4113193415316"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="14.14%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.1 "><p id="p754815413532"><a name="p754815413532"></a><a name="p754815413532"></a><strong id="b11572114355314"><a name="b11572114355314"></a><a name="b11572114355314"></a>邻接表</strong></p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="64.96%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.2 "><p id="p211383405313"><a name="p211383405313"></a><a name="p211383405313"></a>主要出现在图论和计算机科学领域中。邻接表是一种用于表示图的数据结构，其中每个顶点都存储一个链接列表或数组，列出与其相邻的所有顶点。</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.43%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.3 " style="display:none"><p id="p2113113417532"><a name="p2113113417532"></a><a name="p2113113417532"></a>HDK</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.94%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.4 " style="display:none"><p id="p1448012919541"><a name="p1448012919541"></a><a name="p1448012919541"></a>陈先雨</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="7.53%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.5 " style="display:none"><p id="p84471686261"><a name="p84471686261"></a><a name="p84471686261"></a>是</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row2089174110419"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="14.14%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.1 "><p id="p88912412049"><a name="p88912412049"></a><a name="p88912412049"></a><strong id="b1153571719369"><a name="b1153571719369"></a><a name="b1153571719369"></a>LoRA</strong></p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="64.96%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.2 "><p id="p2089114113416"><a name="p2089114113416"></a><a name="p2089114113416"></a>Low-Rank Adaptation，低秩自适应。是一种高效的参数微调方法。</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.43%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.3 " style="display:none"><p id="p198918417418"><a name="p198918417418"></a><a name="p198918417418"></a>MindIE</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.94%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.4 " style="display:none"><p id="p7891541540"><a name="p7891541540"></a><a name="p7891541540"></a>谢昌秀</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="7.53%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.5 " style="display:none"><p id="p88918417414"><a name="p88918417414"></a><a name="p88918417414"></a>是</p>
</td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>

## M<a name="section7604193113357"></a>

<a name="table8560136113918"></a>
<table><thead align="left"><tr id="row256003623911"><th class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="14.14%" id="mcps1.1.6.1.1"><p id="p660718528392"><a name="p660718528392"></a><a name="p660718528392"></a><strong>术语/缩略语</strong></p>
</th>
<th class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="64.96%" id="mcps1.1.6.1.2"><p id="p1960775211395"><a name="p1960775211395"></a><a name="p1960775211395"></a><strong>含义</strong></p>
</th>
<th class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.419999999999999%" id="mcps1.1.6.1.3" style="display:none"><p id="p1660745218392"><a name="p1660745218392"></a><a name="p1660745218392"></a>领域</p>
</th>
<th class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.959999999999999%" id="mcps1.1.6.1.4" style="display:none"><p id="p460795215394"><a name="p460795215394"></a><a name="p460795215394"></a>负责人</p>
</th>
<th class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="7.5200000000000005%" id="mcps1.1.6.1.5" style="display:none"><p id="p203711109262"><a name="p203711109262"></a><a name="p203711109262"></a>是否确认一致</p>
</th>
</tr>
</thead>
<tbody><tr id="row153512351828"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="14.14%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.1 "><p id="p16123171064411"><a name="p16123171064411"></a><a name="p16123171064411"></a><strong id="b6549142319446"><a name="b6549142319446"></a><a name="b6549142319446"></a>MAC</strong></p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="64.96%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.2 "><p id="p6123810114414"><a name="p6123810114414"></a><a name="p6123810114414"></a>Media Access Control，媒体接入控制。</p>
<p id="p1158945214471"><a name="p1158945214471"></a><a name="p1158945214471"></a>媒体接入控制是一种数据链路层协议，用于管理多个设备共享同一传输介质的访问方式，避免冲突。</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.419999999999999%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.3 " style="display:none"><p id="p11231910114419"><a name="p11231910114419"></a><a name="p11231910114419"></a>HDK</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.959999999999999%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.4 " style="display:none"><p id="p11123181020443"><a name="p11123181020443"></a><a name="p11123181020443"></a>陈先雨</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="7.5200000000000005%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.5 " style="display:none"><p id="p137260102616"><a name="p137260102616"></a><a name="p137260102616"></a>是</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row85617364396"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="14.14%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.1 "><p id="p83957598594"><a name="p83957598594"></a><a name="p83957598594"></a><strong id="b930016113246"><a name="b930016113246"></a><a name="b930016113246"></a>Max Absolute Error</strong></p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="64.96%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.2 "><p id="p176751127202616"><a name="p176751127202616"></a><a name="p176751127202616"></a>最大绝对误差算法。</p>
<p id="p113951059155915"><a name="p113951059155915"></a><a name="p113951059155915"></a>精度比对算法之一，计算结果取值范围为0到无穷大，值越接近于0，表明越相近，值越大，表明差距越大。</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.419999999999999%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.3 " style="display:none"><p id="p666916134380"><a name="p666916134380"></a><a name="p666916134380"></a>MindStudio</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.959999999999999%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.4 " style="display:none"><p id="p18345131512614"><a name="p18345131512614"></a><a name="p18345131512614"></a>蔡伟伟</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="7.5200000000000005%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.5 " style="display:none"><p id="p884816141508"><a name="p884816141508"></a><a name="p884816141508"></a>否，待加入术语库</p>
</td>
</tr>

</td>

<p id="p1431483545112"><a name="p1431483545112"></a><a name="p1431483545112"></a>精度比对算法之一，计算结果取值范围为0到无穷大，值越接近于0，表明越相近，值越大，表明差距越大。</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.419999999999999%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.3 " style="display:none"><p id="p186697136388"><a name="p186697136388"></a><a name="p186697136388"></a>MindStudio</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.959999999999999%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.4 " style="display:none"><p id="p5345141511260"><a name="p5345141511260"></a><a name="p5345141511260"></a>蔡伟伟</p>
</td>

</td>
</tr>

</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="64.96%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.2 "><p id="p15571716205919"><a name="p15571716205919"></a><a name="p15571716205919"></a>Micro Controller Unit，微控制器单元。</p>
<p id="p1841516574595"><a name="p1841516574595"></a><a name="p1841516574595"></a>一种集成电路，集成了处理器、存储器和输入输出接口等多种功能模块。</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.419999999999999%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.3 " style="display:none"><p id="p16912319145915"><a name="p16912319145915"></a><a name="p16912319145915"></a>HDK/MindCluster</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.959999999999999%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.4 " style="display:none"><p id="p391361945913"><a name="p391361945913"></a><a name="p391361945913"></a>陈先雨/胡可茹</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="7.5200000000000005%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.5 " style="display:none"><p id="p437219015263"><a name="p437219015263"></a><a name="p437219015263"></a>是</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row156223673917"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="14.14%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.1 "><p id="p94629522525"><a name="p94629522525"></a><a name="p94629522525"></a><strong id="b10330411132416"><a name="b10330411132416"></a><a name="b10330411132416"></a>Mean Absolute Error</strong></p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="64.96%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.2 "><p id="p8339535132711"><a name="p8339535132711"></a><a name="p8339535132711"></a>平均绝对误差算法。</p>
<p id="p11343022121915"><a name="p11343022121915"></a><a name="p11343022121915"></a>精度比对算法之一，计算结果取值范围为0到无穷大。</p>
<a name="ul9710134671918"></a><a name="ul9710134671918"></a><ul id="ul9710134671918"><li>MeanAbsoluteError趋于0，RootMeanSquareError趋于0，说明测量值与真实值越近似。</li><li>MeanAbsoluteError趋于0，RootMeanSquareError越大，说明存在局部过大的异常值。</li><li>MeanAbsoluteError越大，RootMeanSquareError等于或近似MeanAbsoluteError，说明整体偏差越集中。</li><li>MeanAbsoluteError越大，RootMeanSquareError越大于MeanAbsoluteError，说明存在整体偏差，且整体偏差分布分散。</li><li>不存在以上情况的例外情况，因为RMSE ≥ MAE恒成立。</li></ul>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.419999999999999%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.3 " style="display:none"><p id="p866941353810"><a name="p866941353810"></a><a name="p866941353810"></a>MindStudio</p>
</td>

</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="7.5200000000000005%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.5 " style="display:none"><p id="p485317497515"><a name="p485317497515"></a><a name="p485317497515"></a>否，待更新术语库</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row4563636103911"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="14.14%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.1 "><p id="p546511527529"><a name="p546511527529"></a><a name="p546511527529"></a><strong id="b63328119247"><a name="b63328119247"></a><a name="b63328119247"></a>Mean Relative Error</strong></p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="64.96%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.2 "><p id="p11842115119270"><a name="p11842115119270"></a><a name="p11842115119270"></a>平均相对误差算法。</p>
<p id="p35291232205113"><a name="p35291232205113"></a><a name="p35291232205113"></a>精度比对算法之一，计算结果取值范围为0到无穷大，值越接近于0，表明越相近，值越大，表明差距越大。</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.419999999999999%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.3 " style="display:none"><p id="p13669111317388"><a name="p13669111317388"></a><a name="p13669111317388"></a>MindStudio</p>
</td>

</td>

</td>
</tr>

<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="7.5200000000000005%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.5 " style="display:none"><p id="p737117062612"><a name="p737117062612"></a><a name="p737117062612"></a>是</p>
</td>

<tr id="row187751220164718"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="14.14%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.1 "><p id="p1675415225512"><a name="p1675415225512"></a><a name="p1675415225512"></a><strong id="b1918382965112"><a name="b1918382965112"></a><a name="b1918382965112"></a>MindIE</strong></p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="64.96%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.2 "><p id="p1475492285120"><a name="p1475492285120"></a><a name="p1475492285120"></a>Mind Inference Engine，昇腾推理引擎。基于昇腾硬件的运行加速、调试调优、快速迁移部署的高性能深度学习推理框架，分层开放满足各类需求，统一接口使能极简开发，沉淀能力构筑极致性能。</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.419999999999999%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.3 " style="display:none"><p id="p1875422255112"><a name="p1875422255112"></a><a name="p1875422255112"></a>MindIE</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.959999999999999%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.4 " style="display:none"><p id="p1975412265120"><a name="p1975412265120"></a><a name="p1975412265120"></a>肖庆</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="7.5200000000000005%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.5 " style="display:none"><p id="p037119092611"><a name="p037119092611"></a><a name="p037119092611"></a>是</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row19286172424710"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="14.14%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.1 "><p id="p5154173362616"><a name="p5154173362616"></a><a name="p5154173362616"></a><strong id="b175531444112619"><a name="b175531444112619"></a><a name="b175531444112619"></a>MindFormers</strong></p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="64.96%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.2 "><p id="p19413045117"><a name="p19413045117"></a><a name="p19413045117"></a>MindSpore Transformers，基于MindSpore框架的大模型全流程套件。</p>
<p id="p0154233172610"><a name="p0154233172610"></a><a name="p0154233172610"></a>支持大模型训练、微调、评估、推理和部署。</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.419999999999999%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.3 " style="display:none"><p id="p18154333132614"><a name="p18154333132614"></a><a name="p18154333132614"></a>MindIE</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.959999999999999%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.4 " style="display:none"><p id="p14154173320263"><a name="p14154173320263"></a><a name="p14154173320263"></a>谢昌秀</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="7.5200000000000005%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.5 " style="display:none"><p id="p5371208264"><a name="p5371208264"></a><a name="p5371208264"></a>否，待加入术语库</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row19468152424718"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="14.14%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.1 "><p id="p24705102254"><a name="p24705102254"></a><a name="p24705102254"></a><strong id="b192979397554"><a name="b192979397554"></a><a name="b192979397554"></a>MindIO</strong></p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="64.96%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.2 "><p id="p144701010132519"><a name="p144701010132519"></a><a name="p144701010132519"></a>内存缓存系统，可以提升训练Checkpoint读写速度。</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.419999999999999%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.3 " style="display:none"><p id="p74703106253"><a name="p74703106253"></a><a name="p74703106253"></a>MindCluster集群调度</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.959999999999999%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.4 " style="display:none"><p id="p54701110152513"><a name="p54701110152513"></a><a name="p54701110152513"></a>瞿铁铮</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="7.5200000000000005%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.5 " style="display:none"><p id="p1237116012613"><a name="p1237116012613"></a><a name="p1237116012613"></a>是</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row1464103694313"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="14.14%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.1 "><p id="p16443616433"><a name="p16443616433"></a><a name="p16443616433"></a>MinIO</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="64.96%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.2 "><p id="p1464123617433"><a name="p1464123617433"></a><a name="p1464123617433"></a>基础对象存储服务组件。</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.419999999999999%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.3 " style="display:none"><p id="p10391153715439"><a name="p10391153715439"></a><a name="p10391153715439"></a>Mind Edge</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.959999999999999%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.4 " style="display:none"><p id="p839193794315"><a name="p839193794315"></a><a name="p839193794315"></a>陆嘉怡</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="7.5200000000000005%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.5 " style="display:none"><p id="p337110082619"><a name="p337110082619"></a><a name="p337110082619"></a>是</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row8269322836"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="14.14%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.1 "><p id="p111772040173911"><a name="p111772040173911"></a><a name="p111772040173911"></a><strong id="b170546134415"><a name="b170546134415"></a><a name="b170546134415"></a>MLP</strong></p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="64.96%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.2 "><p id="p14177124063920"><a name="p14177124063920"></a><a name="p14177124063920"></a><span>Multilayer Perceptron</span>，多层感知器。</p>
<p id="p55897151452"><a name="p55897151452"></a><a name="p55897151452"></a>多层感知器<span>是一种前向结构的</span>人工神经网络<span>，它由输入层、一个或多个隐藏层和输出层组成。</span>MLP可以用于解决各种问题，如分类、回归等，由于其强大的表达能力，MLP在许多领域中都有广泛的应用，例如图像识别、自然语言处理等。</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.419999999999999%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.3 " style="display:none"><p id="p2265144982911"><a name="p2265144982911"></a><a name="p2265144982911"></a>PTA</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.959999999999999%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.4 " style="display:none"><p id="p3367019218"><a name="p3367019218"></a><a name="p3367019218"></a>刘倩</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="7.5200000000000005%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.5 " style="display:none"><p id="p83717042614"><a name="p83717042614"></a><a name="p83717042614"></a>是</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row15565236153918"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="14.14%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.1 "><p id="p3423558611"><a name="p3423558611"></a><a name="p3423558611"></a><strong id="b11583754191312"><a name="b11583754191312"></a><a name="b11583754191312"></a>MoE</strong></p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="64.96%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.2 "><p id="p204231451164"><a name="p204231451164"></a><a name="p204231451164"></a>Mixture-of-Experts，混合专家系统，是一种用于训练万亿参数量级模型的技术。MoE将预测建模任务分解为若干子任务，在每个子任务上训练一个专家模型（Expert Model），开发一个门控模型（Gating Model），该模型会根据输入数据分配一个或多个专家，最终综合多个专家计算结果作为预测结果。</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.419999999999999%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.3 " style="display:none"><p id="p766915130387"><a name="p766915130387"></a><a name="p766915130387"></a>CANN</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.959999999999999%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.4 " style="display:none"><p id="p734517152268"><a name="p734517152268"></a><a name="p734517152268"></a>陈娇</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="7.5200000000000005%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.5 " style="display:none"><p id="p20371008268"><a name="p20371008268"></a><a name="p20371008268"></a>是</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row1426812541476"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="14.14%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.1 "><p id="p1226819542473"><a name="p1226819542473"></a><a name="p1226819542473"></a><strong id="b438751184817"><a name="b438751184817"></a><a name="b438751184817"></a>msDebug</strong></p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="64.96%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.2 "><p id="p2026845494716"><a name="p2026845494716"></a><a name="p2026845494716"></a>算子调试。</p>
<p id="p101613917482"><a name="p101613917482"></a><a name="p101613917482"></a>提供基于昇腾处理器的原生环境调试能力，实现灵活的变量展示。支持算子功能调试，单步调试（上板&仿真）等功能。</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.419999999999999%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.3 " style="display:none"><p id="p15250101824812"><a name="p15250101824812"></a><a name="p15250101824812"></a>MindStudio</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.959999999999999%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.4 " style="display:none"><p id="p425012185483"><a name="p425012185483"></a><a name="p425012185483"></a>李翠</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="7.5200000000000005%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.5 " style="display:none"><p id="p13250121884811"><a name="p13250121884811"></a><a name="p13250121884811"></a>是</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row1965121118461"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="14.14%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.1 "><p id="p17651191119466"><a name="p17651191119466"></a><a name="p17651191119466"></a><strong id="b6323131714619"><a name="b6323131714619"></a><a name="b6323131714619"></a>msKPP</strong></p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="64.96%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.2 "><p id="p1565141144617"><a name="p1565141144617"></a><a name="p1565141144617"></a>算子设计。算子理论性能建模和模板库算子性能调优工具。</p>
<p id="p1433485460"><a name="p1433485460"></a><a name="p1433485460"></a>在性能建模阶段，工具会内置算子API性能数据，支持用户在设计阶段表达算子实现算法并评估性能。</p>
<p id="p43184817462"><a name="p43184817462"></a><a name="p43184817462"></a>在模板库算子性能调优阶段，提供模板库Kernel下发代码生成、编译、运行的能力，同时提供Kernel内代码替换并自动调优的能力。</p>
</td>

</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row985922516485"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="14.14%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.1 "><p id="p1985972524812"><a name="p1985972524812"></a><a name="p1985972524812"></a><strong id="b25951032194813"><a name="b25951032194813"></a><a name="b25951032194813"></a>msProf</strong></p>

<p id="p1875173920487"><a name="p1875173920487"></a><a name="p1875173920487"></a>提供上板和仿真的性能数据采集方式，并通过MindStudio Insight进行可视化呈现，方便用户快速定位算子性能瓶颈。</p>
</td>

</td>
</tr>

</td>

</td>
</tr>

</td>

<p id="p14953104717475"><a name="p14953104717475"></a><a name="p14953104717475"></a>算子异常检测工具，提供内存检测和竞争检测能力，支持多核程序下的内存问题的精准定位。</p>
</td>

</td>

</td>

</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row26372053141917"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="14.14%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.1 "><p id="p1419663613919"><a name="p1419663613919"></a><a name="p1419663613919"></a><strong id="b195941453193914"><a name="b195941453193914"></a><a name="b195941453193914"></a>MTE</strong></p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="64.96%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.2 "><p id="p13196636143918"><a name="p13196636143918"></a><a name="p13196636143918"></a>Memory Transfer Engine，内存传输引擎。</p>
<p id="p6884172815408"><a name="p6884172815408"></a><a name="p6884172815408"></a>MTE也被称作LSU（Load Store Unit），负责AICore内部数据在不同Buffer之间的读写管理，以及完成一些格式转换的操作。</p>
</td>

</td>

</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="7.5200000000000005%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.5 " style="display:none"><p id="p1837117032615"><a name="p1837117032615"></a><a name="p1837117032615"></a>是</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row35666368390"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="14.14%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.1 "><p id="p15742086"><a name="p15742086"></a><a name="p15742086"></a><strong id="b18563191275214"><a name="b18563191275214"></a><a name="b18563191275214"></a>MTE1</strong></p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="64.96%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.2 "><p id="p2365728115117"><a name="p2365728115117"></a><a name="p2365728115117"></a>Memory Transfer Engine 1，AI Core的数据传递引擎，负责将数据从L1 Buffer搬运到L0A  Buffer或L0B Buffer等。注意：不同硬件能力可能有差异。</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.419999999999999%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.3 " style="display:none"><p id="p1466921383818"><a name="p1466921383818"></a><a name="p1466921383818"></a>CANN</p>
</td>

</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="7.5200000000000005%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.5 " style="display:none"><p id="p173718015268"><a name="p173718015268"></a><a name="p173718015268"></a>否，待更新术语库</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row16567123693916"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="14.14%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.1 "><p id="p55244468"><a name="p55244468"></a><a name="p55244468"></a><strong id="b856415124527"><a name="b856415124527"></a><a name="b856415124527"></a>MTE2</strong></p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="64.96%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.2 "><p id="p73651928205114"><a name="p73651928205114"></a><a name="p73651928205114"></a>Memory Transfer Engine 2，AI Core的数据传递引擎，负责将数据从GM搬运到L1 Buffer、L0A Buffer、L0B Buffer、Unified Buffer等。注意：不同硬件能力可能有差异。</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.419999999999999%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.3 " style="display:none"><p id="p1866981320389"><a name="p1866981320389"></a><a name="p1866981320389"></a>CANN</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.959999999999999%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.4 " style="display:none"><p id="p1345115122617"><a name="p1345115122617"></a><a name="p1345115122617"></a>牟楠</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="7.5200000000000005%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.5 " style="display:none"><p id="p1537113010264"><a name="p1537113010264"></a><a name="p1537113010264"></a>否，待更新术语库</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row12567163623917"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="14.14%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.1 "><p id="p64164835"><a name="p64164835"></a><a name="p64164835"></a><strong id="b65641512175216"><a name="b65641512175216"></a><a name="b65641512175216"></a>MTE3</strong></p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="64.96%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.2 "><p id="p203651128105117"><a name="p203651128105117"></a><a name="p203651128105117"></a>Memory Transfer Engine 3，AI Core的数据传递引擎，负责将数据从Unified Buffer搬运到Global Memory、L1 Buffer等。注意：不同硬件能力可能有差异。</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.419999999999999%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.3 " style="display:none"><p id="p10398615163818"><a name="p10398615163818"></a><a name="p10398615163818"></a>CANN</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.959999999999999%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.4 " style="display:none"><p id="p1934511532616"><a name="p1934511532616"></a><a name="p1934511532616"></a>牟楠</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="7.5200000000000005%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.5 " style="display:none"><p id="p16371804262"><a name="p16371804262"></a><a name="p16371804262"></a>否，待更新术语库</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row11463143771117"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="14.14%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.1 "><p id="p67692474513"><a name="p67692474513"></a><a name="p67692474513"></a><strong id="b1394119334458"><a name="b1394119334458"></a><a name="b1394119334458"></a>MTU</strong></p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="64.96%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.2 "><p id="p57697474520"><a name="p57697474520"></a><a name="p57697474520"></a>Maximum Transmission Unit，最大传输单元。</p>
<p id="p13293165643313"><a name="p13293165643313"></a><a name="p13293165643313"></a>在网络中能够传输的最大数据报文。大小根据网络可变，如在X.25网络中是576字节，以太网中是1500字节，16Mbit/s令牌环中是17914字节。MTU大小取决于网络的链路层。当报文在网络上传输时，路径MTU，即PMTU确定了相关网络中最小报文尺寸，即所有网络能够不分段传输的报文大小。</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.419999999999999%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.3 " style="display:none"><p id="p312373724515"><a name="p312373724515"></a><a name="p312373724515"></a>HDK</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.959999999999999%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.4 " style="display:none"><p id="p51231437174514"><a name="p51231437174514"></a><a name="p51231437174514"></a>陈先雨</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="7.5200000000000005%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.5 " style="display:none"><p id="p137213042616"><a name="p137213042616"></a><a name="p137213042616"></a>是</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row103181616058"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="14.14%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.1 "><p id="p1431811161057"><a name="p1431811161057"></a><a name="p1431811161057"></a><strong id="b17341446123618"><a name="b17341446123618"></a><a name="b17341446123618"></a>MindIE SD</strong></p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="64.96%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.2 "><p id="p10318121610517"><a name="p10318121610517"></a><a name="p10318121610517"></a>Mind Inference Engine Stable Diffusion，Diffusion系列大模型，是MindIE的视图生成推理模型套件。</p>
</td>

</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.959999999999999%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.4 " style="display:none"><p id="p3318616451"><a name="p3318616451"></a><a name="p3318616451"></a>肖庆</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="7.5200000000000005%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.5 " style="display:none"><p id="p1231891612519"><a name="p1231891612519"></a><a name="p1231891612519"></a>否，待加入术语库</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row19944192414518"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="14.14%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.1 "><p id="p4944102411513"><a name="p4944102411513"></a><a name="p4944102411513"></a><strong id="b1874534613616"><a name="b1874534613616"></a><a name="b1874534613616"></a>MindIE Turbo</strong></p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="64.96%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.2 "><p id="p189444241658"><a name="p189444241658"></a><a name="p189444241658"></a>Mind Inference Engine Turbo，昇腾推理引擎加速插件库，是在昇腾硬件上开发的LLM推理引擎加速插件库。</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.419999999999999%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.3 " style="display:none"><p id="p19944202419516"><a name="p19944202419516"></a><a name="p19944202419516"></a>MindIE</p>
</td>

</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="7.5200000000000005%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.5 " style="display:none"><p id="p13944152417514"><a name="p13944152417514"></a><a name="p13944152417514"></a>否，待加入术语库</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row1393283514511"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="14.14%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.1 "><p id="p693218359519"><a name="p693218359519"></a><a name="p693218359519"></a><strong id="b10758144617364"><a name="b10758144617364"></a><a name="b10758144617364"></a>MindIE Motor</strong></p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="64.96%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.2 "><p id="p1393273510519"><a name="p1393273510519"></a><a name="p1393273510519"></a>Mind Inference Engine Motor，是面向LLM PD分离推理的请求调度框架，通过开放、可扩展的推理服务化平台架构提供推理服务化能力，向下对接<span id="ph385115414288"><a name="ph385115414288"></a><a name="ph385115414288"></a>MindIE LLM</span>，满足大语言模型高性能推理需求。</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.419999999999999%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.3 " style="display:none"><p id="p10932113512515"><a name="p10932113512515"></a><a name="p10932113512515"></a>MindIE</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.959999999999999%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.4 " style="display:none"><p id="p2093317351156"><a name="p2093317351156"></a><a name="p2093317351156"></a>肖庆</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="7.5200000000000005%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.5 " style="display:none"><p id="p129331352514"><a name="p129331352514"></a><a name="p129331352514"></a>否，待加入术语库</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row724313396517"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="14.14%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.1 "><p id="p1243123912518"><a name="p1243123912518"></a><a name="p1243123912518"></a><strong id="b575924633615"><a name="b575924633615"></a><a name="b575924633615"></a>MindIE LLM</strong></p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="64.96%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.2 "><p id="p2243183919514"><a name="p2243183919514"></a><a name="p2243183919514"></a>Mind Inference Engine Large Language Model，大语言模型，是MindIE下的大语言模型推理组件。</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.419999999999999%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.3 " style="display:none"><p id="p14243133918515"><a name="p14243133918515"></a><a name="p14243133918515"></a>MindIE</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.959999999999999%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.4 " style="display:none"><p id="p13243123912519"><a name="p13243123912519"></a><a name="p13243123912519"></a>谢昌秀</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="7.5200000000000005%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.5 " style="display:none"><p id="p182438391551"><a name="p182438391551"></a><a name="p182438391551"></a>否，待加入术语库</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row9603162411710"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="14.14%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.1 "><p id="p1260352412717"><a name="p1260352412717"></a><a name="p1260352412717"></a><strong id="b2759846173618"><a name="b2759846173618"></a><a name="b2759846173618"></a>MLA</strong></p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="64.96%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.2 "><p id="p166034249719"><a name="p166034249719"></a><a name="p166034249719"></a>Multi-head Latent Attention，多头潜在注意力。这是一种高效的注意力机制，利用低秩键值联合压缩来消除推理时键值缓存的瓶颈，从而支持高效推理。</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.419999999999999%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.3 " style="display:none"><p id="p1060311241577"><a name="p1060311241577"></a><a name="p1060311241577"></a>MindIE</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.959999999999999%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.4 " style="display:none"><p id="p106033241375"><a name="p106033241375"></a><a name="p106033241375"></a>谢昌秀</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="7.5200000000000005%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.5 " style="display:none"><p id="p06038246714"><a name="p06038246714"></a><a name="p06038246714"></a>是</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row1093319338714"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="14.14%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.1 "><p id="p1693312332711"><a name="p1693312332711"></a><a name="p1693312332711"></a><strong id="b16760154614361"><a name="b16760154614361"></a><a name="b16760154614361"></a>MTP</strong></p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="64.96%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.2 "><p id="p189342033676"><a name="p189342033676"></a><a name="p189342033676"></a>Multi-Token Prediction，多Token预测。这是DeepSeek中提出的一种用于单次生成多个token的并行解码方法。核心思想是在推理过程中，模型不仅预测下一个token，而且会同时预测多个token，从而显著提升模型生成速度。</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.419999999999999%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.3 " style="display:none"><p id="p1893453318716"><a name="p1893453318716"></a><a name="p1893453318716"></a>MindIE</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.959999999999999%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.4 " style="display:none"><p id="p59341033478"><a name="p59341033478"></a><a name="p59341033478"></a>谢昌秀</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="7.5200000000000005%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.5 " style="display:none"><p id="p209348333719"><a name="p209348333719"></a><a name="p209348333719"></a>否，需要更新华为术语库</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row1541514285719"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="14.14%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.1 "><p id="p541562813717"><a name="p541562813717"></a><a name="p541562813717"></a><strong id="b3761546183614"><a name="b3761546183614"></a><a name="b3761546183614"></a>MindIE Service Tools</strong></p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="64.96%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.2 "><p id="p144158281473"><a name="p144158281473"></a><a name="p144158281473"></a>昇腾推理服务化工具，主要功能有大模型推理性能测试、精度测试、可视化以及自动寻优的能力，并且支持通过配置提升吞吐。</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.419999999999999%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.3 " style="display:none"><p id="p34151828974"><a name="p34151828974"></a><a name="p34151828974"></a>MindIE</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.959999999999999%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.4 " style="display:none"><p id="p74159281475"><a name="p74159281475"></a><a name="p74159281475"></a>肖庆</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="7.5200000000000005%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.5 " style="display:none"><p id="p341517281875"><a name="p341517281875"></a><a name="p341517281875"></a>否，待加入术语库</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row174221737874"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="14.14%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.1 "><p id="p11422193710718"><a name="p11422193710718"></a><a name="p11422193710718"></a><strong id="b1476244613615"><a name="b1476244613615"></a><a name="b1476244613615"></a>MindIE Simulator</strong></p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="64.96%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.2 "><p id="p8422137175"><a name="p8422137175"></a><a name="p8422137175"></a>服务化性能自动调优，通过多种算法模拟策略，在满足时延约束下寻找最优调参策略提升服务性能。</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.419999999999999%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.3 " style="display:none"><p id="p14226371076"><a name="p14226371076"></a><a name="p14226371076"></a>MindIE</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.959999999999999%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.4 " style="display:none"><p id="p134229378718"><a name="p134229378718"></a><a name="p134229378718"></a>肖庆</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="7.5200000000000005%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.5 " style="display:none"><p id="p342211374713"><a name="p342211374713"></a><a name="p342211374713"></a>否，待加入术语库</p>
</td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>

<a name="table7127628124017"></a>

<th class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.950000000000001%" id="mcps1.1.6.1.4" style="display:none"><p id="p1247846194015"><a name="p1247846194015"></a><a name="p1247846194015"></a>负责人</p>
</th>

</th>

<tbody><tr id="row71276286400"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="14.14%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.1 "><p id="p242810310499"><a name="p242810310499"></a><a name="p242810310499"></a><strong id="b464893814918"><a name="b464893814918"></a><a name="b464893814918"></a>NCS</strong></p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="64.96%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.2 "><p id="p14428183113494"><a name="p14428183113494"></a><a name="p14428183113494"></a>Neural Compute Server，神经计算服务器。</p>
<p id="p1121125917497"><a name="p1121125917497"></a><a name="p1121125917497"></a>NCS封装AscendCL运行服务接口，可接受外部远程上板请求，并且返回对应的性能数据。</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.43%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.3 " style="display:none"><p id="p18398515103811"><a name="p18398515103811"></a><a name="p18398515103811"></a>CANN</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.950000000000001%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.4 " style="display:none"><p id="p1734615158261"><a name="p1734615158261"></a><a name="p1734615158261"></a>刘晓芳</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="7.5200000000000005%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.5 " style="display:none"><p id="p086104392918"><a name="p086104392918"></a><a name="p086104392918"></a>是</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row21281328124018"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="14.14%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.1 "><p id="p17611282"><a name="p17611282"></a><a name="p17611282"></a><strong id="b11162132385017"><a name="b11162132385017"></a><a name="b11162132385017"></a>NIC</strong></p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="64.96%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.2 "><p id="p17227723"><a name="p17227723"></a><a name="p17227723"></a>Network Interface Controller，网络接口控制器。</p>
<p id="p19766715"><a name="p19766715"></a><a name="p19766715"></a>也称为网络接口卡，网络适配器，LAN适配器，以及类似术语。是将计算机连接到计算机网络的计算机硬件组件。</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.43%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.3 " style="display:none"><p id="p63986151380"><a name="p63986151380"></a><a name="p63986151380"></a>CANN</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.950000000000001%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.4 " style="display:none"><p id="p163463159268"><a name="p163463159268"></a><a name="p163463159268"></a>陈萍萍/李闫珍</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="7.5200000000000005%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.5 " style="display:none"><p id="p41631449185514"><a name="p41631449185514"></a><a name="p41631449185514"></a>是</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row36284138418"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="14.14%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.1 "><p id="p13987354133010"><a name="p13987354133010"></a><a name="p13987354133010"></a><strong id="b2655483120"><a name="b2655483120"></a><a name="b2655483120"></a>NLP</strong></p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="64.96%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.2 "><p id="p1879211712311"><a name="p1879211712311"></a><a name="p1879211712311"></a>Natural Language Processing，自然语言处理。</p>
<p id="p498735483014"><a name="p498735483014"></a><a name="p498735483014"></a><span>是</span>人工智能<span>和</span>语言学<span>领域的分支学科。此领域探讨如何处理及运用</span>自然语言<span>，自然语言处理包括多方面和步骤，基本有认知、理解、生成等部分。</span></p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.43%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.3 " style="display:none"><p id="p83852310331"><a name="p83852310331"></a><a name="p83852310331"></a>PTA</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.950000000000001%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.4 " style="display:none"><p id="p173892310333"><a name="p173892310333"></a><a name="p173892310333"></a>刘倩</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="7.5200000000000005%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.5 " style="display:none"><p id="p15871443172911"><a name="p15871443172911"></a><a name="p15871443172911"></a>是</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row111281228164015"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="14.14%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.1 "><p id="p47408069"><a name="p47408069"></a><a name="p47408069"></a><strong id="b188321624205014"><a name="b188321624205014"></a><a name="b188321624205014"></a>NN</strong></p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="64.96%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.2 "><p id="p14848410"><a name="p14848410"></a><a name="p14848410"></a>Neural Network，神经网络。</p>
<p id="p45461382"><a name="p45461382"></a><a name="p45461382"></a>在机器学习和认知科学领域，是一种模仿生物神经网络的结构和功能的数学模型或计算模型。</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.43%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.3 " style="display:none"><p id="p2039891523818"><a name="p2039891523818"></a><a name="p2039891523818"></a>CANN</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.950000000000001%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.4 " style="display:none"><p id="p734641522610"><a name="p734641522610"></a><a name="p734641522610"></a>陈娇</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="7.5200000000000005%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.5 " style="display:none"><p id="p28614435298"><a name="p28614435298"></a><a name="p28614435298"></a>是</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row4129102854013"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="14.14%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.1 "><p id="p27481582"><a name="p27481582"></a><a name="p27481582"></a><strong id="b576117269501"><a name="b576117269501"></a><a name="b576117269501"></a>NPU</strong></p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="64.96%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.2 "><p id="p536512288518"><a name="p536512288518"></a><a name="p536512288518"></a>Neural-Network Processing Unit，神经网络处理器单元。采用“数据驱动并行计算”的架构，专门用于处理人工智能应用中的大量计算任务。</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.43%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.3 " style="display:none"><p id="p1839815154389"><a name="p1839815154389"></a><a name="p1839815154389"></a>CANN</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.950000000000001%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.4 " style="display:none"><p id="p334691519266"><a name="p334691519266"></a><a name="p334691519266"></a>臧燕</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="7.5200000000000005%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.5 " style="display:none"><p id="p1218410469501"><a name="p1218410469501"></a><a name="p1218410469501"></a>是</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row1437880363"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="14.14%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.1 "><p id="p126993632514"><a name="p126993632514"></a><a name="p126993632514"></a><strong id="b10446135512256"><a name="b10446135512256"></a><a name="b10446135512256"></a>NUMA</strong></p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="64.96%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.2 "><p id="p18486175214574"><a name="p18486175214574"></a><a name="p18486175214574"></a>Non-Uniform Memory Access，非一致性内存访问。</p>
<p id="p12426131717585"><a name="p12426131717585"></a><a name="p12426131717585"></a>NUMA模式是一种分布式存储器访问方式，处理器可以同时访问不同的存储器地址，大幅度提高并行性。该模式下，处理器被划分成多个“节点”（node），每个节点分配一块本地存储器空间。所有节点中的处理器都可以访问全部的物理存储器，但是访问本节点内的存储器所需要的时间，比访问某些远程节点内的存储器所花的时间要少得多。</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.43%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.3 " style="display:none"><p id="p167013622512"><a name="p167013622512"></a><a name="p167013622512"></a>MindIE</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.950000000000001%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.4 " style="display:none"><p id="p18701436112510"><a name="p18701436112510"></a><a name="p18701436112510"></a>谢昌秀</p>
<p id="p03158112466"><a name="p03158112466"></a><a name="p03158112466"></a>瞿铁铮</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="7.5200000000000005%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.5 " style="display:none"><p id="p1187114313299"><a name="p1187114313299"></a><a name="p1187114313299"></a>是</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row1224230162910"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="14.14%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.1 "><p id="p239212016493"><a name="p239212016493"></a><a name="p239212016493"></a><strong id="b63681029154916"><a name="b63681029154916"></a><a name="b63681029154916"></a>NVMe</strong></p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="64.96%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.2 "><p id="p1392182094911"><a name="p1392182094911"></a><a name="p1392182094911"></a>Non-Volatile Memory Express，非易失性内存主机控制器接口规范。</p>
<p id="p957191117504"><a name="p957191117504"></a><a name="p957191117504"></a><span>是一个逻辑设备接口</span>规范<span>。它是基于设备逻辑接口的</span>总线<span>传输协议规范（相当于通讯协议中的</span>应用层<span>），用于访问通过</span>PCI Express<span>（PCIe）总线附加的</span>非易失性存储器<span>介质（例如采用</span>闪存<span>的</span>固态硬盘驱动器<span>）。</span></p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.43%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.3 " style="display:none"><p id="p142423022918"><a name="p142423022918"></a><a name="p142423022918"></a>PTA</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.950000000000001%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.4 " style="display:none"><p id="p19243307297"><a name="p19243307297"></a><a name="p19243307297"></a>刘倩</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="7.5200000000000005%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.5 " style="display:none"><p id="p1875436290"><a name="p1875436290"></a><a name="p1875436290"></a>是</p>
</td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>

## O<a name="section17845131123517"></a>

<a name="table14672101174115"></a>
<table><thead align="left"><tr id="row96732113415"><th class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="14.14%" id="mcps1.1.6.1.1"><p id="p164686814210"><a name="p164686814210"></a><a name="p164686814210"></a><strong>术语/缩略语</strong></p>
</th>
<th class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="64.96%" id="mcps1.1.6.1.2"><p id="p12468168134218"><a name="p12468168134218"></a><a name="p12468168134218"></a><strong>含义</strong></p>
</th>
<th class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.43%" id="mcps1.1.6.1.3" style="display:none"><p id="p1746818804211"><a name="p1746818804211"></a><a name="p1746818804211"></a>领域</p>
</th>
<th class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.950000000000001%" id="mcps1.1.6.1.4" style="display:none"><p id="p7469138184217"><a name="p7469138184217"></a><a name="p7469138184217"></a>负责人</p>
</th>
<th class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="7.5200000000000005%" id="mcps1.1.6.1.5" style="display:none"><p id="p1043015586291"><a name="p1043015586291"></a><a name="p1043015586291"></a>是否确认一致</p>
</th>
</tr>
</thead>
<tbody><tr id="row6752558124512"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="14.14%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.1 "><p id="p127414599452"><a name="p127414599452"></a><a name="p127414599452"></a><strong id="b411741418183"><a name="b411741418183"></a><a name="b411741418183"></a>OM</strong></p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="64.96%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.2 "><p id="p0741185918453"><a name="p0741185918453"></a><a name="p0741185918453"></a>offline model，离线模型。</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.43%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.3 " style="display:none"><p id="p1774155954516"><a name="p1774155954516"></a><a name="p1774155954516"></a>Mind Edge</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.950000000000001%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.4 " style="display:none"><p id="p157414592455"><a name="p157414592455"></a><a name="p157414592455"></a>陆嘉怡</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="7.5200000000000005%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.5 " style="display:none"><p id="p124301458112918"><a name="p124301458112918"></a><a name="p124301458112918"></a>是</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row8232057124511"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="14.14%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.1 "><p id="p07411859184518"><a name="p07411859184518"></a><a name="p07411859184518"></a><strong id="b177411599453"><a name="b177411599453"></a><a name="b177411599453"></a>ONNX</strong></p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="64.96%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.2 "><p id="p13116125154816"><a name="p13116125154816"></a><a name="p13116125154816"></a>Open Neural Network Exchange，<span>开放神经网络交换。</span></p>
<p id="p167411259124511"><a name="p167411259124511"></a><a name="p167411259124511"></a><span>ONNX是一种针对机器学习所设计的开放式的文件格式，用于存储训练好的模型。它使得不同的人工智能框架可以采用相同格式存储模型数据并交互。</span></p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.43%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.3 " style="display:none"><p id="p47413591459"><a name="p47413591459"></a><a name="p47413591459"></a>PTA</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.950000000000001%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.4 " style="display:none"><p id="p87411595456"><a name="p87411595456"></a><a name="p87411595456"></a>刘倩</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="7.5200000000000005%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.5 " style="display:none"><p id="p7430195882912"><a name="p7430195882912"></a><a name="p7430195882912"></a>是</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row96999417198"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="14.14%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.1 "><p id="p183329520562"><a name="p183329520562"></a><a name="p183329520562"></a><strong id="b777315201838"><a name="b777315201838"></a><a name="b777315201838"></a>OOM</strong></p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="64.96%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.2 "><p id="p103325512564"><a name="p103325512564"></a><a name="p103325512564"></a>Out Of Memory，内存不足。</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.43%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.3 " style="display:none"><p id="p1730785661910"><a name="p1730785661910"></a><a name="p1730785661910"></a>MindCluster集群调度</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.950000000000001%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.4 " style="display:none"><p id="p1830715565192"><a name="p1830715565192"></a><a name="p1830715565192"></a>瞿铁铮</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="7.5200000000000005%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.5 " style="display:none"><p id="p18430155812916"><a name="p18430155812916"></a><a name="p18430155812916"></a>是</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row16673111204119"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="14.14%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.1 "><p id="p66441544"><a name="p66441544"></a><a name="p66441544"></a><strong id="b163031371527"><a name="b163031371527"></a><a name="b163031371527"></a>OP</strong></p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="64.96%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.2 "><p id="p12355171320142"><a name="p12355171320142"></a><a name="p12355171320142"></a>算子（Operator，简称OP），是深度学习算法中执行特定数学运算或操作的基础单元，例如激活函数（如ReLU）、卷积（Conv）、池化（Pooling）以及归一化（如Softmax）。通过组合这些算子，可以构建神经网络模型。</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.43%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.3 " style="display:none"><p id="p1398715133819"><a name="p1398715133819"></a><a name="p1398715133819"></a>CANN</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.950000000000001%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.4 " style="display:none"><p id="p934617153264"><a name="p934617153264"></a><a name="p934617153264"></a>牟楠</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="7.5200000000000005%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.5 " style="display:none"><p id="p11128141010366"><a name="p11128141010366"></a><a name="p11128141010366"></a>否</p>
<p id="p6129151020362"><a name="p6129151020362"></a><a name="p6129151020362"></a>待录入</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row16731411114115"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="14.14%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.1 "><p id="p4689726436"><a name="p4689726436"></a><a name="p4689726436"></a><strong id="b16301201115435"><a name="b16301201115435"></a><a name="b16301201115435"></a>OPAT</strong></p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="64.96%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.2 "><p id="p86899214315"><a name="p86899214315"></a><a name="p86899214315"></a>Operator Auto Tuning，算子自动调优。</p>
<p id="p5546537104316"><a name="p5546537104316"></a><a name="p5546537104316"></a>OPAT是一种提升算子性能的优化器。AOE将一张整图输入给OPAT，OPAT内部进行算子融合，将融合得到的图进行算子粒度切分，针对每一个融合算子子图生成不同的算子调优策略，从而实现最优的算子性能，并将得到的最优策略保存在算子知识库。</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.43%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.3 " style="display:none"><p id="p539891513815"><a name="p539891513815"></a><a name="p539891513815"></a>CANN</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.950000000000001%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.4 " style="display:none"><p id="p1134601513266"><a name="p1134601513266"></a><a name="p1134601513266"></a>刘晓芳</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="7.5200000000000005%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.5 " style="display:none"><p id="p6430195822916"><a name="p6430195822916"></a><a name="p6430195822916"></a>是</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row1731043542617"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="14.14%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.1 "><p id="p838611110487"><a name="p838611110487"></a><a name="p838611110487"></a><strong id="b19386201114818"><a name="b19386201114818"></a><a name="b19386201114818"></a>OpenPGP</strong></p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="64.96%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.2 "><p id="p1038681144811"><a name="p1038681144811"></a><a name="p1038681144811"></a>Open Pretty Good Privacy，开放优良保密协议<strong id="b15386917480"><a name="b15386917480"></a><a name="b15386917480"></a>。</strong></p>
<p id="p13386161114813"><a name="p13386161114813"></a><a name="p13386161114813"></a>PGP（Pretty Good Privacy）是一个加密程序，为数据通信提供了加密和验证功能。PGP通常用于签名、加密和解密文本、电子邮件和文件。OpenPGP是一种非专有协议，为加密消息、签名、私钥和用于交换公钥的证书定义了统一标准。</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.43%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.3 " style="display:none"><p id="p1276911311044"><a name="p1276911311044"></a><a name="p1276911311044"></a>MindCluster集群调度</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.950000000000001%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.4 " style="display:none"><p id="p376910311244"><a name="p376910311244"></a><a name="p376910311244"></a>瞿铁铮</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="7.5200000000000005%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.5 " style="display:none"><p id="p12430105818296"><a name="p12430105818296"></a><a name="p12430105818296"></a>是</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row166741411154113"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="14.14%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.1 "><p id="p1322619120264"><a name="p1322619120264"></a><a name="p1322619120264"></a><strong id="b183049718527"><a name="b183049718527"></a><a name="b183049718527"></a>OPP</strong></p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="64.96%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.2 "><p id="p722612114262"><a name="p722612114262"></a><a name="p722612114262"></a>Operator Package，算子库。</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.43%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.3 " style="display:none"><p id="p1398171510382"><a name="p1398171510382"></a><a name="p1398171510382"></a>CANN</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.950000000000001%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.4 " style="display:none"><p id="p2346161542617"><a name="p2346161542617"></a><a name="p2346161542617"></a>牟楠</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="7.5200000000000005%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.5 " style="display:none"><p id="p115822318587"><a name="p115822318587"></a><a name="p115822318587"></a>否，待更新术语库</p>
</td>

</td>

<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="7.5200000000000005%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.5 " style="display:none"><p id="p1543095820298"><a name="p1543095820298"></a><a name="p1543095820298"></a>是</p>

</td>

</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="64.96%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.2 "><p id="p133862018481"><a name="p133862018481"></a><a name="p133862018481"></a>Open Container Initiative，开放容器计划。</p>
<p id="p338611164816"><a name="p338611164816"></a><a name="p338611164816"></a>Linux基金会于2015年6月成立OCI组织，旨在围绕容器格式和运行时制定一个开放的工业化标准。</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.43%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.3 " style="display:none"><p id="p15383172017314"><a name="p15383172017314"></a><a name="p15383172017314"></a>MindCluster集群调度</p>

</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="7.5200000000000005%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.5 " style="display:none"><p id="p743045882910"><a name="p743045882910"></a><a name="p743045882910"></a>是</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row7807131491111"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="14.14%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.1 "><p id="p08082141116"><a name="p08082141116"></a><a name="p08082141116"></a><strong id="b145731383720"><a name="b145731383720"></a><a name="b145731383720"></a>OM Adapter</strong></p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="64.96%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.2 "><p id="p178081914101112"><a name="p178081914101112"></a><a name="p178081914101112"></a>将 MindIE 的心跳、告警、资源信息和日志等上报至外部告警管控平台，实现服务状态监控与管控平台对接功能。</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.43%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.3 " style="display:none"><p id="p1180821413115"><a name="p1180821413115"></a><a name="p1180821413115"></a>MindIE</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.950000000000001%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.4 " style="display:none"><p id="p12808191471116"><a name="p12808191471116"></a><a name="p12808191471116"></a>肖庆</p>
</td>

</td>
</tr>

<p id="p24889743"><a name="p24889743"></a><a name="p24889743"></a>一种串行外设扩展总线标准，常用于计算机系统中的外设扩展。</p>

</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.950000000000001%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.4 " style="display:none"><p id="p815174916539"><a name="p815174916539"></a><a name="p815174916539"></a>陈先雨</p>
</td>

</td>
