</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="64.96%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.2 "><p id="p111754915535"><a name="p111754915535"></a><a name="p111754915535"></a>Priority-based Flow Control，基于优先级的流量控制。</p>
<p id="p141854913532"><a name="p141854913532"></a><a name="p141854913532"></a>一种基于优先级的流控机制。</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.43%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.3 " style="display:none"><p id="p191811492531"><a name="p191811492531"></a><a name="p191811492531"></a>HDK</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.950000000000001%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.4 " style="display:none"><p id="p131884975320"><a name="p131884975320"></a><a name="p131884975320"></a>陈先雨</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="7.5200000000000005%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.5 " style="display:none"><p id="p1118124915539"><a name="p1118124915539"></a><a name="p1118124915539"></a>是</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row156015535214"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="14.14%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.1 "><p id="p131814912532"><a name="p131814912532"></a><a name="p131814912532"></a><strong id="b9185499535"><a name="b9185499535"></a><a name="b9185499535"></a>PMU</strong></p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="64.96%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.2 "><p id="p171884945319"><a name="p171884945319"></a><a name="p171884945319"></a>Performance Monitor Unit，性能监视单元。</p>
<p id="p81819499531"><a name="p81819499531"></a><a name="p81819499531"></a>PMU是CPU提供的一个单元，属于硬件的范畴。PMU通过访问相关的寄存器能读取到CPU的一些性能数据。</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.43%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.3 " style="display:none"><p id="p191814975316"><a name="p191814975316"></a><a name="p191814975316"></a>CANN</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.950000000000001%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.4 " style="display:none"><p id="p1019134914534"><a name="p1019134914534"></a><a name="p1019134914534"></a>李闫珍</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="7.5200000000000005%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.5 " style="display:none"><p id="p1819164935317"><a name="p1819164935317"></a><a name="p1819164935317"></a>是</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row10726115545210"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="14.14%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.1 "><p id="p201919491535"><a name="p201919491535"></a><a name="p201919491535"></a><strong id="b191974918531"><a name="b191974918531"></a><a name="b191974918531"></a>PNGD</strong></p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="64.96%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.2 "><p id="p1219144995312"><a name="p1219144995312"></a><a name="p1219144995312"></a>PNG Decoder，PNG图像解码器。</p>
<p id="p71914945319"><a name="p71914945319"></a><a name="p71914945319"></a>提供对PNG格式的图像进行解码的能力。</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.43%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.3 " style="display:none"><p id="p191912498539"><a name="p191912498539"></a><a name="p191912498539"></a>CANN</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.950000000000001%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.4 " style="display:none"><p id="p2020124975318"><a name="p2020124975318"></a><a name="p2020124975318"></a>叶成美</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="7.5200000000000005%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.5 " style="display:none"><p id="p12019498533"><a name="p12019498533"></a><a name="p12019498533"></a>否</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row11892135525214"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="14.14%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.1 "><p id="p520204911535"><a name="p520204911535"></a><a name="p520204911535"></a><strong id="b320549145320"><a name="b320549145320"></a><a name="b320549145320"></a>Pod</strong></p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="64.96%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.2 "><p id="p920249105312"><a name="p920249105312"></a><a name="p920249105312"></a>Pod是Kubernetes中可以创建的最小部署单元，也是Kubernetes REST API中的顶级资源类型。</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.43%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.3 " style="display:none"><p id="p520849155313"><a name="p520849155313"></a><a name="p520849155313"></a>MindCluster集群调度</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.950000000000001%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.4 " style="display:none"><p id="p32174995316"><a name="p32174995316"></a><a name="p32174995316"></a>瞿铁铮</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="7.5200000000000005%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.5 " style="display:none"><p id="p172154911539"><a name="p172154911539"></a><a name="p172154911539"></a>是</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row145265615219"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="14.14%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.1 "><p id="p1121124917533"><a name="p1121124917533"></a><a name="p1121124917533"></a><strong id="b1321144925315"><a name="b1321144925315"></a><a name="b1321144925315"></a>Prometheus</strong></p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="64.96%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.2 "><p id="p11211349145319"><a name="p11211349145319"></a><a name="p11211349145319"></a>Prometheus是一个开源系统，能够提供一套产品化的、用来量化管理技术、业务的解决方案。</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.43%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.3 " style="display:none"><p id="p1322164920536"><a name="p1322164920536"></a><a name="p1322164920536"></a>MindCluster集群调度</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.950000000000001%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.4 " style="display:none"><p id="p922114910537"><a name="p922114910537"></a><a name="p922114910537"></a>瞿铁铮</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="7.5200000000000005%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.5 " style="display:none"><p id="p2221491536"><a name="p2221491536"></a><a name="p2221491536"></a>是</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row155254562525"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="14.14%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.1 "><p id="p9241449175311"><a name="p9241449175311"></a><a name="p9241449175311"></a><strong id="b1224194925314"><a name="b1224194925314"></a><a name="b1224194925314"></a>PP</strong></p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="64.96%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.2 "><p id="p024154919533"><a name="p024154919533"></a><a name="p024154919533"></a>Pipeline Parallelism，流水线并行。</p>
<p id="p132494911532"><a name="p132494911532"></a><a name="p132494911532"></a>流水线并行将模型的不同层放置到不同的计算设备，降低单个计算设备的显存消耗，从而实现超大规模模型训练。</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.43%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.3 " style="display:none"><p id="p1724649145317"><a name="p1724649145317"></a><a name="p1724649145317"></a>PTA</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.950000000000001%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.4 " style="display:none"><p id="p1024184975314"><a name="p1024184975314"></a><a name="p1024184975314"></a>刘倩</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="7.5200000000000005%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.5 " style="display:none"><p id="p42484912532"><a name="p42484912532"></a><a name="p42484912532"></a>是（无详细定义）</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row1969018560524"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="14.14%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.1 "><p id="p2251849125319"><a name="p2251849125319"></a><a name="p2251849125319"></a><strong id="b6254490535"><a name="b6254490535"></a><a name="b6254490535"></a>PWM</strong></p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="64.96%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.2 "><p id="p2025249175320"><a name="p2025249175320"></a><a name="p2025249175320"></a>Pulse Width Modulation，脉冲宽度调制。</p>
<p id="p1225134995312"><a name="p1225134995312"></a><a name="p1225134995312"></a>脉冲载波的脉冲持续时间脉宽随调制波的样值而变的脉冲调制方式。</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.43%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.3 " style="display:none"><p id="p725174905317"><a name="p725174905317"></a><a name="p725174905317"></a>CANN</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.950000000000001%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.4 " style="display:none"><p id="p172544912530"><a name="p172544912530"></a><a name="p172544912530"></a>叶成美</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="7.5200000000000005%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.5 " style="display:none"><p id="p32544985318"><a name="p32544985318"></a><a name="p32544985318"></a>是</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row585810564520"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="14.14%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.1 "><p id="p102514913539"><a name="p102514913539"></a><a name="p102514913539"></a><strong id="b126194985317"><a name="b126194985317"></a><a name="b126194985317"></a>片上内存</strong></p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="64.96%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.2 "><p id="p22644985310"><a name="p22644985310"></a><a name="p22644985310"></a>集成在微处理器芯片上的存储器。</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.43%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.3 " style="display:none"><p id="p122674911539"><a name="p122674911539"></a><a name="p122674911539"></a>HDK</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.950000000000001%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.4 " style="display:none"><p id="p92616499530"><a name="p92616499530"></a><a name="p92616499530"></a>陈先雨</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="7.5200000000000005%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.5 " style="display:none"><p id="p1326114915311"><a name="p1326114915311"></a><a name="p1326114915311"></a>是</p>
</td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>

## Q<a name="section6466918134911"></a>

<a name="table17507843145412"></a>
<table><thead align="left"><tr id="row115081143115415"><th class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="14.14%" id="mcps1.1.6.1.1"><p id="p18508164314548"><a name="p18508164314548"></a><a name="p18508164314548"></a><strong>术语/缩略语</strong></p>
</th>
<th class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="64.96%" id="mcps1.1.6.1.2"><p id="p1950844313546"><a name="p1950844313546"></a><a name="p1950844313546"></a><strong>含义</strong></p>
</th>
<th class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.43%" id="mcps1.1.6.1.3" style="display:none"><p id="p950874314541"><a name="p950874314541"></a><a name="p950874314541"></a>领域</p>
</th>
<th class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.950000000000001%" id="mcps1.1.6.1.4" style="display:none"><p id="p195089435546"><a name="p195089435546"></a><a name="p195089435546"></a>负责人</p>
</th>
<th class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="7.5200000000000005%" id="mcps1.1.6.1.5" style="display:none"><p id="p45081943185410"><a name="p45081943185410"></a><a name="p45081943185410"></a>是否确认一致</p>
</th>
</tr>
</thead>
<tbody><tr id="row55081643145412"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="14.14%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.1 "><p id="p176214494542"><a name="p176214494542"></a><a name="p176214494542"></a><strong id="b062164955416"><a name="b062164955416"></a><a name="b062164955416"></a>QAT</strong></p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="64.96%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.2 "><p id="p17621049115413"><a name="p17621049115413"></a><a name="p17621049115413"></a>Quantization-Aware Training，量化感知训练。</p>
<p id="p36211498541"><a name="p36211498541"></a><a name="p36211498541"></a>在重训练过程中引入量化，通过重训练提高模型对量化效应的能力，从而获得更高的量化模型精度的一种量化方式。</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.43%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.3 " style="display:none"><p id="p196364918540"><a name="p196364918540"></a><a name="p196364918540"></a>CANN</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.950000000000001%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.4 " style="display:none"><p id="p1263649105411"><a name="p1263649105411"></a><a name="p1263649105411"></a>陈萍萍</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="7.5200000000000005%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.5 " style="display:none"><p id="p1663174912542"><a name="p1663174912542"></a><a name="p1663174912542"></a>3ms上没有</p>
</td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>

## R<a name="section1178210539484"></a>

<a name="table1226211445513"></a>
<table><thead align="left"><tr id="row13263344552"><th class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="14.14%" id="mcps1.1.6.1.1"><p id="p1926316425510"><a name="p1926316425510"></a><a name="p1926316425510"></a><strong>术语/缩略语</strong></p>
</th>
<th class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="64.96%" id="mcps1.1.6.1.2"><p id="p1826314445515"><a name="p1826314445515"></a><a name="p1826314445515"></a><strong>含义</strong></p>
</th>
<th class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.43%" id="mcps1.1.6.1.3" style="display:none"><p id="p162633425515"><a name="p162633425515"></a><a name="p162633425515"></a>领域</p>
</th>
<th class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.950000000000001%" id="mcps1.1.6.1.4" style="display:none"><p id="p17263154115514"><a name="p17263154115514"></a><a name="p17263154115514"></a>负责人</p>
</th>
<th class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="7.5200000000000005%" id="mcps1.1.6.1.5" style="display:none"><p id="p1626324125518"><a name="p1626324125518"></a><a name="p1626324125518"></a>是否确认一致</p>
</th>
</tr>
</thead>
<tbody><tr id="row192659412557"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="14.14%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.1 "><p id="p1843013183551"><a name="p1843013183551"></a><a name="p1843013183551"></a><strong id="b743013181554"><a name="b743013181554"></a><a name="b743013181554"></a>RDMA</strong></p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="64.96%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.2 "><p id="p1243016187556"><a name="p1243016187556"></a><a name="p1243016187556"></a>Remote Direct Memory Access，远程直接内存访问技术，它<span>将数据直接从一台机器的内存传输到另一台机器，无需双方操作系统的介入，</span>一般指可以跨过网络的内存访问方式。</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.43%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.3 " style="display:none"><p id="p15430191855512"><a name="p15430191855512"></a><a name="p15430191855512"></a>CANN</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.950000000000001%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.4 " style="display:none"><p id="p5430131817557"><a name="p5430131817557"></a><a name="p5430131817557"></a>臧燕/蔡伟伟</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="7.5200000000000005%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.5 " style="display:none"><p id="p194301318115512"><a name="p194301318115512"></a><a name="p194301318115512"></a>是</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row8265194105510"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="14.14%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.1 "><p id="p1243017185552"><a name="p1243017185552"></a><a name="p1243017185552"></a><strong id="b4430151815518"><a name="b4430151815518"></a><a name="b4430151815518"></a>Relative Euclidean Distance</strong></p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="64.96%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.2 "><p id="p174311918155514"><a name="p174311918155514"></a><a name="p174311918155514"></a>欧氏相对距离算法。</p>
<p id="p18431318135510"><a name="p18431318135510"></a><a name="p18431318135510"></a>精度比对算法之一，计算结果取值范围为0到无穷大，值越接近于0，表明越相近，值越大，表明差距越大。</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.43%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.3 " style="display:none"><p id="p4431111811552"><a name="p4431111811552"></a><a name="p4431111811552"></a>MindStudio</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.950000000000001%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.4 " style="display:none"><p id="p1443113182556"><a name="p1443113182556"></a><a name="p1443113182556"></a>蔡伟伟</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="7.5200000000000005%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.5 " style="display:none"><p id="p18431118125512"><a name="p18431118125512"></a><a name="p18431118125512"></a>否，暂不加入华为术语库</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row1926604205516"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="14.14%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.1 "><p id="p1243119183556"><a name="p1243119183556"></a><a name="p1243119183556"></a><strong id="b1843111185559"><a name="b1843111185559"></a><a name="b1843111185559"></a>RoCE</strong></p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="64.96%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.2 "><p id="p194311118165518"><a name="p194311118165518"></a><a name="p194311118165518"></a>RDMA over Converged Ethernet，RDMA融合以太网。</p>
<p id="p1432111895511"><a name="p1432111895511"></a><a name="p1432111895511"></a>RoCE是一种网络协议，允许通过以太网使用远程直接内存访问（RDMA）。目前存在两个RoCE版本，分别是RoCE v1和v2。RoCE v1是数据链路层协议，允许在同一个以太网广播域内的任意两台主机之间通信。RoCE v2是网络层协议，其报文可以被路由。</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.43%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.3 " style="display:none"><p id="p13432141825519"><a name="p13432141825519"></a><a name="p13432141825519"></a>CANN/MindIE/MindStudio</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.950000000000001%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.4 " style="display:none"><p id="p443241895513"><a name="p443241895513"></a><a name="p443241895513"></a>李闫珍/肖庆/蔡伟伟</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="7.5200000000000005%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.5 " style="display:none"><p id="p1143216183550"><a name="p1143216183550"></a><a name="p1143216183550"></a>是</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row12267114195519"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="14.14%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.1 "><p id="p12432018155512"><a name="p12432018155512"></a><a name="p12432018155512"></a><strong id="b9432918135511"><a name="b9432918135511"></a><a name="b9432918135511"></a>Root Mean Square Error</strong></p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="64.96%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.2 "><p id="p5432118145515"><a name="p5432118145515"></a><a name="p5432118145515"></a>均方根误差算法。</p>
<p id="p04321018185519"><a name="p04321018185519"></a><a name="p04321018185519"></a>精度比对算法之一，计算结果取值范围为0到无穷大。</p>
<a name="ul2432201835515"></a><a name="ul2432201835515"></a><ul id="ul2432201835515"><li>MeanAbsoluteError趋于0，RootMeanSquareError趋于0，说明测量值与真实值越近似。</li><li>MeanAbsoluteError趋于0，RootMeanSquareError越大，说明存在局部过大的异常值。</li><li>MeanAbsoluteError越大，RootMeanSquareError等于或近似MeanAbsoluteError，说明整体偏差越集中。</li><li>MeanAbsoluteError越大，RootMeanSquareError越大于MeanAbsoluteError，说明存在整体偏差，且整体偏差分布分散。</li><li>不存在以上情况的例外情况，因为RMSE ≥ MAE恒成立。</li></ul>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.43%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.3 " style="display:none"><p id="p104331918175519"><a name="p104331918175519"></a><a name="p104331918175519"></a>MindStudio</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.950000000000001%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.4 " style="display:none"><p id="p243311815558"><a name="p243311815558"></a><a name="p243311815558"></a>蔡伟伟</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="7.5200000000000005%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.5 " style="display:none"><p id="p543410181553"><a name="p543410181553"></a><a name="p543410181553"></a>否，待更新术语库</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row1426874195518"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="14.14%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.1 "><p id="p5434141816551"><a name="p5434141816551"></a><a name="p5434141816551"></a><strong id="b4434818105517"><a name="b4434818105517"></a><a name="b4434818105517"></a>Runtime</strong></p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="64.96%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.2 "><p id="p7434111814559"><a name="p7434111814559"></a><a name="p7434111814559"></a>为应用程序提供了针对昇腾AI处理器的内存管理、Device管理、Stream管理、Event管理、Kernel加载与执行等功能。</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.43%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.3 " style="display:none"><p id="p1243481812552"><a name="p1243481812552"></a><a name="p1243481812552"></a>CANN</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.950000000000001%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.4 " style="display:none"><p id="p17434191875519"><a name="p17434191875519"></a><a name="p17434191875519"></a>叶成美</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="7.5200000000000005%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.5 " style="display:none"><p id="p1143461845510"><a name="p1143461845510"></a><a name="p1143461845510"></a>是</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row426864125511"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="14.14%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.1 "><p id="p1043431810559"><a name="p1043431810559"></a><a name="p1043431810559"></a><strong id="b12435111814552"><a name="b12435111814552"></a><a name="b12435111814552"></a>RAM</strong></p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="64.96%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.2 "><p id="p44359183555"><a name="p44359183555"></a><a name="p44359183555"></a>Random Access Memory，随机存取存储器。</p>
<p id="p243521817555"><a name="p243521817555"></a><a name="p243521817555"></a>基于半导体的可被CPU或者其他硬件设备读写的内存。可以任何顺序访问存储位置。</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.43%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.3 " style="display:none"><p id="p11435191817553"><a name="p11435191817553"></a><a name="p11435191817553"></a>MindCluster集群调度</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.950000000000001%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.4 " style="display:none"><p id="p443511819555"><a name="p443511819555"></a><a name="p443511819555"></a>瞿铁铮</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="7.5200000000000005%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.5 " style="display:none"><p id="p343511815551"><a name="p343511815551"></a><a name="p343511815551"></a>是</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row1726911435515"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="14.14%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.1 "><p id="p1543512185556"><a name="p1543512185556"></a><a name="p1543512185556"></a><strong id="b54361418165511"><a name="b54361418165511"></a><a name="b54361418165511"></a>runC</strong></p>

<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="64.96%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.2 "><p id="p2436101820552"><a name="p2436101820552"></a><a name="p2436101820552"></a>runC是一个根据OCI（Open Container Initiative）标准创建并运行容器的client工具。</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.43%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.3 " style="display:none"><p id="p15436171810559"><a name="p15436171810559"></a><a name="p15436171810559"></a>MindCluster集群调度</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.950000000000001%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.4 " style="display:none"><p id="p1443691885520"><a name="p1443691885520"></a><a name="p1443691885520"></a>瞿铁铮</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="7.5200000000000005%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.5 " style="display:none"><p id="p5436518165512"><a name="p5436518165512"></a><a name="p5436518165512"></a>是</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row15270843556"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="14.14%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.1 "><p id="p943601813558"><a name="p943601813558"></a><a name="p943601813558"></a><strong id="b243717184553"><a name="b243717184553"></a><a name="b243717184553"></a>RoPE</strong></p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="64.96%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.2 "><p id="p8437191816557"><a name="p8437191816557"></a><a name="p8437191816557"></a>Rotary Position Embedding，旋转式位置嵌入。</p>
<p id="p743751815515"><a name="p743751815515"></a><a name="p743751815515"></a>一种能够将相对位置信息依赖集成到self-attention中并提升transformer架构性能的位置编码方式。</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.43%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.3 " style="display:none"><p id="p154372018155511"><a name="p154372018155511"></a><a name="p154372018155511"></a>PTA</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.950000000000001%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.4 " style="display:none"><p id="p1643761818552"><a name="p1643761818552"></a><a name="p1643761818552"></a>刘倩</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="7.5200000000000005%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.5 " style="display:none"><p id="p9437151865519"><a name="p9437151865519"></a><a name="p9437151865519"></a>是</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row1785651501417"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="14.14%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.1 "><p id="p1785631515143"><a name="p1785631515143"></a><a name="p1785631515143"></a><strong id="b1122141093719"><a name="b1122141093719"></a><a name="b1122141093719"></a>RAS</strong></p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="64.96%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.2 "><p id="p1785661541419"><a name="p1785661541419"></a><a name="p1785661541419"></a>Reliability、Availability和Serviceability。增强PD分离服务可靠性、可用性和可服务性的能力。</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.43%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.3 " style="display:none"><p id="p385661531413"><a name="p385661531413"></a><a name="p385661531413"></a>MindIE</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.950000000000001%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.4 " style="display:none"><p id="p198561415111413"><a name="p198561415111413"></a><a name="p198561415111413"></a>肖庆</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="7.5200000000000005%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.5 " style="display:none"><p id="p20856151515149"><a name="p20856151515149"></a><a name="p20856151515149"></a>否，待加入术语库</p>
</td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>

## S<a name="section16697152820505"></a>

<a name="table4911838175320"></a>
<table><thead align="left"><tr id="row59243813534"><th class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="14.14%" id="mcps1.1.6.1.1"><p id="p11921638205310"><a name="p11921638205310"></a><a name="p11921638205310"></a><strong>术语/缩略语</strong></p>
</th>
<th class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="64.96%" id="mcps1.1.6.1.2"><p id="p59243805316"><a name="p59243805316"></a><a name="p59243805316"></a><strong>含义</strong></p>
</th>
<th class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.43%" id="mcps1.1.6.1.3" style="display:none"><p id="p2926384534"><a name="p2926384534"></a><a name="p2926384534"></a>领域</p>
</th>
<th class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.950000000000001%" id="mcps1.1.6.1.4" style="display:none"><p id="p109333855317"><a name="p109333855317"></a><a name="p109333855317"></a>负责人</p>
</th>
<th class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="7.5200000000000005%" id="mcps1.1.6.1.5" style="display:none"><p id="p2093438125312"><a name="p2093438125312"></a><a name="p2093438125312"></a>是否确认一致</p>
</th>
</tr>
</thead>
<tbody><tr id="row139393818537"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="14.14%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.1 "><p id="p14691711175717"><a name="p14691711175717"></a><a name="p14691711175717"></a><strong id="b104696113572"><a name="b104696113572"></a><a name="b104696113572"></a>Scalar</strong></p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="64.96%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.2 "><p id="p536582815514"><a name="p536582815514"></a><a name="p536582815514"></a>AI Core上的标量计算单元，主要负责标量数据运算和对其他单元（如MTE数据搬运单元、Vector矢量计算单元、Cube矩阵计算单元）的指令发射。</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.43%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.3 " style="display:none"><p id="p15469811145716"><a name="p15469811145716"></a><a name="p15469811145716"></a>CANN</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.950000000000001%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.4 " style="display:none"><p id="p204691111572"><a name="p204691111572"></a><a name="p204691111572"></a>牟楠</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="7.5200000000000005%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.5 " style="display:none"><p id="p168831111813"><a name="p168831111813"></a><a name="p168831111813"></a>是，待上3ms术语库</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row16941838205314"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="14.14%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.1 "><p id="p4470141118578"><a name="p4470141118578"></a><a name="p4470141118578"></a><strong id="b447015113572"><a name="b447015113572"></a><a name="b447015113572"></a>SDMA</strong></p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="64.96%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.2 "><p id="p347001175715"><a name="p347001175715"></a><a name="p347001175715"></a>System Direct Memory Access，系统直接内存访问技术，简称DMA，允许外围设备直接访问系统内存，而不需要CPU的干预。</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.43%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.3 " style="display:none"><p id="p164701211135711"><a name="p164701211135711"></a><a name="p164701211135711"></a>CANN</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.950000000000001%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.4 " style="display:none"><p id="p7470111116572"><a name="p7470111116572"></a><a name="p7470111116572"></a>臧燕</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="7.5200000000000005%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.5 " style="display:none"><p id="p447012114571"><a name="p447012114571"></a><a name="p447012114571"></a>是</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row3212187183315"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="14.14%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.1 "><p id="p13341733152912"><a name="p13341733152912"></a><a name="p13341733152912"></a><strong id="b1646122153310"><a name="b1646122153310"></a><a name="b1646122153310"></a>SiP</strong></p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="64.96%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.2 "><p id="p934533132920"><a name="p934533132920"></a><a name="p934533132920"></a>Ascend Signal Processing Boost，信号处理加速库。</p>
<p id="p158225092414"><a name="p158225092414"></a><a name="p158225092414"></a>信号处理加速库面向AI模型场景（支持Pytorch调用）和信号处理场景（支持C++直调），提供一系列信号处理领域相关的高性能算子。</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.43%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.3 " style="display:none"><p id="p421310753317"><a name="p421310753317"></a><a name="p421310753317"></a>CANN信号加速库</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.950000000000001%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.4 " style="display:none"><p id="p821397163313"><a name="p821397163313"></a><a name="p821397163313"></a>冯英</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="7.5200000000000005%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.5 " style="display:none"><p id="p20213373336"><a name="p20213373336"></a><a name="p20213373336"></a>是</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row209573835316"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="14.14%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.1 "><p id="p17471111115717"><a name="p17471111115717"></a><a name="p17471111115717"></a><strong id="b15471161116570"><a name="b15471161116570"></a><a name="b15471161116570"></a>SGAT</strong></p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="64.96%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.2 "><p id="p154717119570"><a name="p154717119570"></a><a name="p154717119570"></a>SubGraph Auto Tuning，子图自动调优。</p>
<p id="p124711311145717"><a name="p124711311145717"></a><a name="p124711311145717"></a>SGAT是一种提升子图性能的优化器。一张完整的网络，会被拆分成多个子图。针对每一个子图，通过SGAT生成不同的调优策略。SGAT的调优算法通过获取每个迭代的调优策略性能数据，找到最优的调优策略，从而实现对应子图的最优性能。</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.43%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.3 " style="display:none"><p id="p1247181112576"><a name="p1247181112576"></a><a name="p1247181112576"></a>CANN</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.950000000000001%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.4 " style="display:none"><p id="p347131117578"><a name="p347131117578"></a><a name="p347131117578"></a>刘晓芳</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="7.5200000000000005%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.5 " style="display:none"><p id="p13471191135711"><a name="p13471191135711"></a><a name="p13471191135711"></a>是</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row1796113865317"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="14.14%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.1 "><p id="p12473161195718"><a name="p12473161195718"></a><a name="p12473161195718"></a><strong id="b8473101115578"><a name="b8473101115578"></a><a name="b8473101115578"></a>SPI</strong></p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="64.96%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.2 "><p id="p54741111135715"><a name="p54741111135715"></a><a name="p54741111135715"></a>Serial Peripheral Interface，串行外设接口。</p>
<p id="p4474141119578"><a name="p4474141119578"></a><a name="p4474141119578"></a>SPI总线系统是一种同步串行外设接口，它可以使MCU（Microcontroller Unit，微控制单元）与各种外围设备以串行方式进行通信以交换信息。</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.43%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.3 " style="display:none"><p id="p10474111119573"><a name="p10474111119573"></a><a name="p10474111119573"></a>CANN</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.950000000000001%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.4 " style="display:none"><p id="p1447417113577"><a name="p1447417113577"></a><a name="p1447417113577"></a>叶成美</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="7.5200000000000005%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.5 " style="display:none"><p id="p10474181111574"><a name="p10474181111574"></a><a name="p10474181111574"></a>否</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row0971438205316"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="14.14%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.1 "><p id="p184751911135711"><a name="p184751911135711"></a><a name="p184751911135711"></a><strong id="b6475411125714"><a name="b6475411125714"></a><a name="b6475411125714"></a>SP</strong></p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="64.96%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.2 "><p id="p1847511112577"><a name="p1847511112577"></a><a name="p1847511112577"></a>Sequence Parallelism，序列并行。</p>
<p id="p10475101114577"><a name="p10475101114577"></a><a name="p10475101114577"></a>序列并行是一种针对输入序列进行列切分的并行计算方式，它可以在张量并行的基础上进一步提高计算效率。序列并行并不会增加额外的通信量，因此在开启张量并行的同时建议也同步开启序列并行。</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.43%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.3 " style="display:none"><p id="p1547521119579"><a name="p1547521119579"></a><a name="p1547521119579"></a>PTA</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.950000000000001%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.4 " style="display:none"><p id="p1847521118575"><a name="p1847521118575"></a><a name="p1847521118575"></a>刘倩</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="7.5200000000000005%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.5 " style="display:none"><p id="p1616035364914"><a name="p1616035364914"></a><a name="p1616035364914"></a>否</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row1397638165316"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="14.14%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.1 "><p id="p2476191165714"><a name="p2476191165714"></a><a name="p2476191165714"></a><strong id="b9476191165712"><a name="b9476191165712"></a><a name="b9476191165712"></a>SRAM</strong></p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="64.96%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.2 "><p id="p54767119576"><a name="p54767119576"></a><a name="p54767119576"></a>Static Random Access Memory，静态随机存储器。</p>
<p id="p647621155717"><a name="p647621155717"></a><a name="p647621155717"></a>是一种计算机内存类型。与动态随机存储器（DRAM）相比，SRAM的读写速度更快，通常用于高速缓存、寄存器和其他需要快速访问的应用程序。</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.43%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.3 " style="display:none"><p id="p8476171185714"><a name="p8476171185714"></a><a name="p8476171185714"></a>PTA</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.950000000000001%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.4 " style="display:none"><p id="p174762113572"><a name="p174762113572"></a><a name="p174762113572"></a>刘倩</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="7.5200000000000005%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.5 " style="display:none"><p id="p24761114576"><a name="p24761114576"></a><a name="p24761114576"></a>是</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row998163813534"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="14.14%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.1 "><p id="p13477141110572"><a name="p13477141110572"></a><a name="p13477141110572"></a><strong id="b24771511145717"><a name="b24771511145717"></a><a name="b24771511145717"></a>SwiGLU</strong></p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="64.96%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.2 "><p id="p247761145713"><a name="p247761145713"></a><a name="p247761145713"></a><span>Swish-Gated Linear Units，Swish门控线性单元激活函数</span>。</p>
<p id="p8477511145711"><a name="p8477511145711"></a><a name="p8477511145711"></a>是Gated Linear Units函数的一种变体，其融入了Swish激活函数。</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.43%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.3 " style="display:none"><p id="p4477511185713"><a name="p4477511185713"></a><a name="p4477511185713"></a>PTA</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.950000000000001%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.4 " style="display:none"><p id="p19477211115718"><a name="p19477211115718"></a><a name="p19477211115718"></a>刘倩</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="7.5200000000000005%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.5 " style="display:none"><p id="p447771117578"><a name="p447771117578"></a><a name="p447771117578"></a>是</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row199833855318"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="14.14%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.1 "><p id="p104781611175714"><a name="p104781611175714"></a><a name="p104781611175714"></a><strong id="b5478101135720"><a name="b5478101135720"></a><a name="b5478101135720"></a>SIMD</strong></p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="64.96%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.2 "><p id="p16478121117575"><a name="p16478121117575"></a><a name="p16478121117575"></a>Single Instruction，Multiple Data，单指令，多数据。</p>
<p id="p6478811205716"><a name="p6478811205716"></a><a name="p6478811205716"></a>一种并行处理器计算机结构，其中一个指令处理器取得指令，并将其分配给其他一些处理器进行处理。</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.43%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.3 " style="display:none"><p id="p347813117577"><a name="p347813117577"></a><a name="p347813117577"></a>PTA</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.950000000000001%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.4 " style="display:none"><p id="p54783118577"><a name="p54783118577"></a><a name="p54783118577"></a>刘倩</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="7.5200000000000005%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.5 " style="display:none"><p id="p94781011175716"><a name="p94781011175716"></a><a name="p94781011175716"></a>是</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row129923815311"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="14.14%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.1 "><p id="p14783119579"><a name="p14783119579"></a><a name="p14783119579"></a><strong id="b12478511145712"><a name="b12478511145712"></a><a name="b12478511145712"></a>SSL</strong></p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="64.96%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.2 "><p id="p3479511155720"><a name="p3479511155720"></a><a name="p3479511155720"></a>Secure Sockets Layer，安全套接层。</p>
<p id="p12479211115714"><a name="p12479211115714"></a><a name="p12479211115714"></a>工作于套接字层的安全协议。该层位于TCP层和应用层之间，用于数据的加解密以及相关实体的认证。</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.43%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.3 " style="display:none"><p id="p154791110579"><a name="p154791110579"></a><a name="p154791110579"></a>Mind Edge</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.950000000000001%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.4 " style="display:none"><p id="p17479911105713"><a name="p17479911105713"></a><a name="p17479911105713"></a>陆嘉怡</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="7.5200000000000005%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.5 " style="display:none"><p id="p3479911205713"><a name="p3479911205713"></a><a name="p3479911205713"></a>是</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row161001838165311"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="14.14%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.1 "><p id="p19479511165713"><a name="p19479511165713"></a><a name="p19479511165713"></a><strong id="b184796116572"><a name="b184796116572"></a><a name="b184796116572"></a>Standard Deviation</strong></p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="64.96%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.2 "><p id="p1748051125714"><a name="p1748051125714"></a><a name="p1748051125714"></a>标准差算法。</p>
<p id="p11480311115717"><a name="p11480311115717"></a><a name="p11480311115717"></a>精度比对算法之一，计算结果取值范围为0到无穷大。标准差越小，离散度越小，表明越接近平均值。</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.43%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.3 " style="display:none"><p id="p12480611135718"><a name="p12480611135718"></a><a name="p12480611135718"></a>MindStudio</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.950000000000001%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.4 " style="display:none"><p id="p17480101120573"><a name="p17480101120573"></a><a name="p17480101120573"></a>蔡伟伟</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="7.5200000000000005%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.5 " style="display:none"><p id="p4480111105711"><a name="p4480111105711"></a><a name="p4480111105711"></a>否，待更新术语库</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row110012385538"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="14.14%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.1 "><p id="p848071115577"><a name="p848071115577"></a><a name="p848071115577"></a><strong id="b248010115570"><a name="b248010115570"></a><a name="b248010115570"></a>STARS</strong></p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="64.96%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.2 "><p id="p648021175716"><a name="p648021175716"></a><a name="p648021175716"></a>System Task and Resource Scheduler，系统任务和资源调度器。</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.43%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.3 " style="display:none"><p id="p1248111135715"><a name="p1248111135715"></a><a name="p1248111135715"></a>MindStudio</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.950000000000001%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.4 " style="display:none"><p id="p1248161113578"><a name="p1248161113578"></a><a name="p1248161113578"></a>蔡伟伟</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="7.5200000000000005%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.5 " style="display:none"><p id="p7481711105719"><a name="p7481711105719"></a><a name="p7481711105719"></a>否，需要更新术语库</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row161015389534"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="14.14%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.1 "><p id="p7482101135714"><a name="p7482101135714"></a><a name="p7482101135714"></a><strong id="b5482161120578"><a name="b5482161120578"></a><a name="b5482161120578"></a>spine-leaf</strong></p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="64.96%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.2 "><p id="p34821211145718"><a name="p34821211145718"></a><a name="p34821211145718"></a>叶脊网络架构。在leaf-spine架构中有两个重要组件，leaf（叶）交换机和spine（脊）换机。其中spine交换机可以当作传统三层架构中的核心交换机，这里的spine交换机不再是三层架构中的大型机箱式交换机，而是高端口密度的交换机。而leaf交换机就是接入层，leaf交换机提供网络连接给终端、服务器，同时上联给spine交换机。 leaf-spine拓扑网络结构主要为解决数据中心内流量的快速增长和数据中心规模的不断扩大，满足传统的三层网络拓扑结构不能满足的数据中心内部高速互连的需求。</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.43%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.3 " style="display:none"><p id="p7483111125714"><a name="p7483111125714"></a><a name="p7483111125714"></a>MindCluster集群调度</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.950000000000001%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.4 " style="display:none"><p id="p748381115716"><a name="p748381115716"></a><a name="p748381115716"></a>瞿铁铮</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="7.5200000000000005%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.5 " style="display:none"><p id="p348351117574"><a name="p348351117574"></a><a name="p348351117574"></a>是</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row19102173815313"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="14.14%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.1 "><p id="p6483101118576"><a name="p6483101118576"></a><a name="p6483101118576"></a><strong id="b1948314119579"><a name="b1948314119579"></a><a name="b1948314119579"></a>Sample-based</strong></p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="64.96%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.2 "><p id="p1648316118575"><a name="p1648316118575"></a><a name="p1648316118575"></a>Profiling的AICore数据以固定的时间周期（AI Core-Sampling Interval）进行性能数据采集。</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.43%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.3 " style="display:none"><p id="p20484181111574"><a name="p20484181111574"></a><a name="p20484181111574"></a>MindStudio</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.950000000000001%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.4 " style="display:none"><p id="p04846113572"><a name="p04846113572"></a><a name="p04846113572"></a>蔡伟伟</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="7.5200000000000005%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.5 " style="display:none"><p id="p184841211175715"><a name="p184841211175715"></a><a name="p184841211175715"></a>是</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row410314381534"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="14.14%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.1 "><p id="p54849113572"><a name="p54849113572"></a><a name="p54849113572"></a><strong id="b194847117571"><a name="b194847117571"></a><a name="b194847117571"></a>Step Trace</strong></p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="64.96%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.2 "><p id="p1548413119576"><a name="p1548413119576"></a><a name="p1548413119576"></a>迭代轨迹。</p>
<p id="p848401175717"><a name="p848401175717"></a><a name="p848401175717"></a>包含迭代的正、反向计算开始结束时间、梯度更新以及数据增强拖尾阶段。</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.43%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.3 " style="display:none"><p id="p1148421119574"><a name="p1148421119574"></a><a name="p1148421119574"></a>MindStudio</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.950000000000001%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.4 " style="display:none"><p id="p848521135713"><a name="p848521135713"></a><a name="p848521135713"></a>蔡伟伟</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="7.5200000000000005%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.5 " style="display:none"><p id="p9485191185713"><a name="p9485191185713"></a><a name="p9485191185713"></a>否，需要更新华为术语库</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row810314385534"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="14.14%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.1 "><p id="p74851011155719"><a name="p74851011155719"></a><a name="p74851011155719"></a><strong id="b174852118574"><a name="b174852118574"></a><a name="b174852118574"></a>ST</strong></p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="64.96%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.2 "><p id="p1948561165719"><a name="p1948561165719"></a><a name="p1948561165719"></a>System Test，系统测试。</p>
<p id="p16485121135719"><a name="p16485121135719"></a><a name="p16485121135719"></a>系统测试是基于系统整体需求说明书的黑盒类测试，应覆盖系统所有联合的部件。系统测试是针对整个产品系统进行的测试，目的是验证系统是否满足了需求规格的定义，找出与需求规格不相符合或与之矛盾的地方。</p>
<p id="p838716592453"><a name="p838716592453"></a><a name="p838716592453"></a>系统测试的对象不仅仅包括需要测试的产品系统的软件，还要包含软件所依赖的硬件、外设甚至包括某些数据、某些支持软件及其接口等。因此，必须将系统中的软件与各种依赖的资源结合起来，在系统实际运行环境下来进行测试。</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.43%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.3 " style="display:none"><p id="p348561110574"><a name="p348561110574"></a><a name="p348561110574"></a>MindStudio</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.950000000000001%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.4 " style="display:none"><p id="p44868113579"><a name="p44868113579"></a><a name="p44868113579"></a>李翠</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="7.5200000000000005%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.5 " style="display:none"><p id="p17486111115713"><a name="p17486111115713"></a><a name="p17486111115713"></a>是</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row12104193818536"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="14.14%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.1 "><p id="p5486111165719"><a name="p5486111165719"></a><a name="p5486111165719"></a><strong id="b13620751175"><a name="b13620751175"></a><a name="b13620751175"></a>昇腾虚拟化实例</strong></p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="64.96%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.2 "><p id="p862122855113"><a name="p862122855113"></a><a name="p862122855113"></a>指通过资源虚拟化技术将1个NPU切分成若干个vNPU（虚拟NPU）实例挂载到虚拟机或容器中使用，使得1个NPU同时支持多个计算调度任务。AVI通过算力资源切分的方式，实现了资源虚拟化复用并保证了资源的安全隔离，大大降低了用户使用NPU算力的门槛和成本。并且支持多租户按需进行资源申请或回收管理。</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.43%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.3 " style="display:none"><p id="p2486181125710"><a name="p2486181125710"></a><a name="p2486181125710"></a>HDK</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.950000000000001%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.4 " style="display:none"><p id="p648631111578"><a name="p648631111578"></a><a name="p648631111578"></a>陈先雨</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="7.5200000000000005%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.5 " style="display:none"><p id="p6487131112579"><a name="p6487131112579"></a><a name="p6487131112579"></a>否，华为术语库中只有这个词没有详细解释-已刷新</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row15661715171517"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="14.14%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.1 "><p id="p176613159150"><a name="p176613159150"></a><a name="p176613159150"></a><strong id="b10692142112378"><a name="b10692142112378"></a><a name="b10692142112378"></a>SLO</strong></p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="64.96%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.2 "><p id="p46619157153"><a name="p46619157153"></a><a name="p46619157153"></a>Service Level Objective，服务级别目标。指在设定时间段内为特定指标设定的目标值。</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.43%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.3 " style="display:none"><p id="p1583731219331"><a name="p1583731219331"></a><a name="p1583731219331"></a>MindIE</p>
<p id="p191822903212"><a name="p191822903212"></a><a name="p191822903212"></a></p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.950000000000001%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.4 " style="display:none"><p id="p266201531515"><a name="p266201531515"></a><a name="p266201531515"></a>肖庆</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="7.5200000000000005%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.5 " style="display:none"><p id="p1661015181515"><a name="p1661015181515"></a><a name="p1661015181515"></a>是</p>
</td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>

<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="64.96%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.2 "><p id="p16464515582"><a name="p16464515582"></a><a name="p16464515582"></a>Profiling的AICore数据以task为粒度进行性能数据采集。</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.510000000000001%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.3 " style="display:none"><p id="p104641516585"><a name="p104641516585"></a><a name="p104641516585"></a>MindStudio</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.950000000000001%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.4 " style="display:none"><p id="p154648112583"><a name="p154648112583"></a><a name="p154648112583"></a>蔡伟伟</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="7.5200000000000005%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.5 " style="display:none"><p id="p11464181185813"><a name="p11464181185813"></a><a name="p11464181185813"></a>是</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row178256440573"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="14.14%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.1 "><p id="p1646571185816"><a name="p1646571185816"></a><a name="p1646571185816"></a><strong id="b846541115812"><a name="b846541115812"></a><a name="b846541115812"></a>TCP</strong></p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="64.96%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.2 "><p id="p546520175813"><a name="p546520175813"></a><a name="p546520175813"></a>Transmission Control Protocol，传输控制协议。</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.510000000000001%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.3 " style="display:none"><p id="p446581165811"><a name="p446581165811"></a><a name="p446581165811"></a>MindCluster集群调度</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.950000000000001%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.4 " style="display:none"><p id="p194657111584"><a name="p194657111584"></a><a name="p194657111584"></a>瞿铁铮</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="7.5200000000000005%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.5 " style="display:none"><p id="p11466171145817"><a name="p11466171145817"></a><a name="p11466171145817"></a>是</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row17825134415579"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="14.14%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.1 "><p id="p24667195818"><a name="p24667195818"></a><a name="p24667195818"></a><strong id="b1446615110582"><a name="b1446615110582"></a><a name="b1446615110582"></a>TDP</strong></p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="64.96%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.2 "><p id="p17466511586"><a name="p17466511586"></a><a name="p17466511586"></a>Thermal Design Power，散热设计功耗。</p>
<p id="p646617125811"><a name="p646617125811"></a><a name="p646617125811"></a>用于描述处理器或其他电子元件在正常工作时的最大热耗散功率。</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.510000000000001%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.3 " style="display:none"><p id="p1946612118585"><a name="p1946612118585"></a><a name="p1946612118585"></a>HDK</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.950000000000001%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.4 " style="display:none"><p id="p20467121115815"><a name="p20467121115815"></a><a name="p20467121115815"></a>陈先雨</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="7.5200000000000005%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.5 " style="display:none"><p id="p9467310581"><a name="p9467310581"></a><a name="p9467310581"></a>是</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row18261244155716"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="14.14%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.1 "><p id="p1467215583"><a name="p1467215583"></a><a name="p1467215583"></a><strong id="b184675118582"><a name="b184675118582"></a><a name="b184675118582"></a>Tensor</strong></p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="64.96%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.2 "><p id="p11365162845114"><a name="p11365162845114"></a><a name="p11365162845114"></a>Tensor张量是算子计算数据的容器，是N维数据结构，最常见的是标量、矢量或矩阵。张量的元素可以包含整数值、浮点值或字符串值。</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.510000000000001%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.3 " style="display:none"><p id="p1046721135810"><a name="p1046721135810"></a><a name="p1046721135810"></a>CANN</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.950000000000001%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.4 " style="display:none"><p id="p64671510581"><a name="p64671510581"></a><a name="p64671510581"></a>牟楠</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="7.5200000000000005%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.5 " style="display:none"><p id="p174681145814"><a name="p174681145814"></a><a name="p174681145814"></a>否，需要更新华为术语库</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row11828194435719"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="14.14%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.1 "><p id="p1146981195814"><a name="p1146981195814"></a><a name="p1146981195814"></a><strong id="b1146991165816"><a name="b1146991165816"></a><a name="b1146991165816"></a>TFT</strong></p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="64.96%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.2 "><p id="p104691213589"><a name="p104691213589"></a><a name="p104691213589"></a>Training Fault Tolerance，训练容错框架。</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.510000000000001%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.3 " style="display:none"><p id="p12469415589"><a name="p12469415589"></a><a name="p12469415589"></a>MindCluster集群调度</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.950000000000001%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.4 " style="display:none"><p id="p746912175818"><a name="p746912175818"></a><a name="p746912175818"></a>瞿铁铮</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="7.5200000000000005%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.5 " style="display:none"><p id="p1146961185816"><a name="p1146961185816"></a><a name="p1146961185816"></a>是</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row1828164419573"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="14.14%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.1 "><p id="p1346901205810"><a name="p1346901205810"></a><a name="p1346901205810"></a><strong id="b18469812589"><a name="b18469812589"></a><a name="b18469812589"></a>TIK</strong></p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="64.96%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.2 "><p id="p14701119584"><a name="p14701119584"></a><a name="p14701119584"></a>Tensor Iterator Kernel，张量嵌套内核。</p>
<p id="p114705155817"><a name="p114705155817"></a><a name="p114705155817"></a>算子开发方式之一，调用TIK提供的API基于Python语言编写自定义算子，TIK编译器会将其编译为适配昇腾AI处理器应用程序的二进制文件。</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.510000000000001%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.3 " style="display:none"><p id="p6470317580"><a name="p6470317580"></a><a name="p6470317580"></a>MindStudio</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.950000000000001%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.4 " style="display:none"><p id="p84704185813"><a name="p84704185813"></a><a name="p84704185813"></a>李翠</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="7.5200000000000005%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.5 " style="display:none"><p id="p84705111586"><a name="p84705111586"></a><a name="p84705111586"></a>是，待上3ms术语库</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row158291344165718"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="14.14%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.1 "><p id="p447012118582"><a name="p447012118582"></a><a name="p447012118582"></a><strong id="b114709155815"><a name="b114709155815"></a><a name="b114709155815"></a>TGI</strong></p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="64.96%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.2 "><p id="p747110110585"><a name="p747110110585"></a><a name="p747110110585"></a>Text Generation Inference，文本生成推理。</p>
<p id="p447113135818"><a name="p447113135818"></a><a name="p447113135818"></a>TGI是一个用于部署和服务大型语言模型的工具包。TGI为最流行的开源LLM提供高性能文本生成，包括LLaMA、Falcon、StarCoder、BLOOM、GPT-NeoX等。</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.510000000000001%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.3 " style="display:none"><p id="p124711016587"><a name="p124711016587"></a><a name="p124711016587"></a>MindIE</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.950000000000001%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.4 " style="display:none"><p id="p247115185810"><a name="p247115185810"></a><a name="p247115185810"></a>肖庆</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="7.5200000000000005%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.5 " style="display:none"><p id="p124718111583"><a name="p124718111583"></a><a name="p124718111583"></a>否，需要更新华为术语库</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row383004495716"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="14.14%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.1 "><p id="p18471317588"><a name="p18471317588"></a><a name="p18471317588"></a><strong id="b114721110584"><a name="b114721110584"></a><a name="b114721110584"></a>TLS</strong></p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="64.96%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.2 "><p id="p04728195811"><a name="p04728195811"></a><a name="p04728195811"></a>Transport Layer Security，传输层安全性协议。</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.510000000000001%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.3 " style="display:none"><p id="p1747217155813"><a name="p1747217155813"></a><a name="p1747217155813"></a>MindCluster集群调度</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.950000000000001%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.4 " style="display:none"><p id="p747213116589"><a name="p747213116589"></a><a name="p747213116589"></a>瞿铁铮</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="7.5200000000000005%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.5 " style="display:none"><p id="p11473161185815"><a name="p11473161185815"></a><a name="p11473161185815"></a>是</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row483184415718"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="14.14%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.1 "><p id="p13474121125820"><a name="p13474121125820"></a><a name="p13474121125820"></a><strong id="b847518110582"><a name="b847518110582"></a><a name="b847518110582"></a>TP</strong></p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="64.96%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.2 "><p id="p147512175814"><a name="p147512175814"></a><a name="p147512175814"></a>Tensor Parallelism，张量并行。</p>
<p id="p1247514195818"><a name="p1247514195818"></a><a name="p1247514195818"></a>通过将网络中的张量（Tensor）切分到不同的设备，从而降低单个设备的显存消耗，使得超大规模模型训练成为可能。</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.510000000000001%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.3 " style="display:none"><p id="p847513115584"><a name="p847513115584"></a><a name="p847513115584"></a>PTA</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.950000000000001%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.4 " style="display:none"><p id="p13475212584"><a name="p13475212584"></a><a name="p13475212584"></a>刘倩</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="7.5200000000000005%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.5 " style="display:none"><p id="p154755135813"><a name="p154755135813"></a><a name="p154755135813"></a>是</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row14832184425718"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="14.14%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.1 "><p id="p15476111115816"><a name="p15476111115816"></a><a name="p15476111115816"></a><strong id="b84764185818"><a name="b84764185818"></a><a name="b84764185818"></a>Triton</strong></p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="64.96%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.2 "><p id="p34768112582"><a name="p34768112582"></a><a name="p34768112582"></a>Triton Inference Server，开源的推理服务软件。</p>
<p id="p1547661115813"><a name="p1547661115813"></a><a name="p1547661115813"></a>通过Triton可以在基于GPU或CPU的各种基础架构（云、数据中心或边缘）上部署、运行和扩展来自任何框架的AI模型。</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.510000000000001%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.3 " style="display:none"><p id="p24765125814"><a name="p24765125814"></a><a name="p24765125814"></a>MindIE</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.950000000000001%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.4 " style="display:none"><p id="p547619117581"><a name="p547619117581"></a><a name="p547619117581"></a>肖庆</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="7.5200000000000005%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.5 " style="display:none"><p id="p154764195818"><a name="p154764195818"></a><a name="p154764195818"></a>是</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row19833544115713"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="14.14%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.1 "><p id="p64771515588"><a name="p64771515588"></a><a name="p64771515588"></a><strong id="b174774113584"><a name="b174774113584"></a><a name="b174774113584"></a>TTP</strong></p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="64.96%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.2 "><p id="p1447714118588"><a name="p1447714118588"></a><a name="p1447714118588"></a>Try To Persist，临终Checkpoint保存。</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.510000000000001%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.3 " style="display:none"><p id="p19477913584"><a name="p19477913584"></a><a name="p19477913584"></a>MindCluster集群调度</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.950000000000001%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.4 " style="display:none"><p id="p134771410589"><a name="p134771410589"></a><a name="p134771410589"></a>瞿铁铮</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="7.5200000000000005%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.5 " style="display:none"><p id="p19477181135818"><a name="p19477181135818"></a><a name="p19477181135818"></a>是</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row98411820145219"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="14.14%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.1 "><p id="p11842320105214"><a name="p11842320105214"></a><a name="p11842320105214"></a><strong id="b1515592710378"><a name="b1515592710378"></a><a name="b1515592710378"></a>TPOT</strong></p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="64.96%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.2 "><p id="p1842202013526"><a name="p1842202013526"></a><a name="p1842202013526"></a>Time per output token，每个输出token的延迟（不含首个token）。每个输出token的延迟（不含首个token）。在离线的批处理应用中，TPOT是最重要的指标，因为它决定了整个推理过程的时间。</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.510000000000001%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.3 " style="display:none"><p id="p18842182015528"><a name="p18842182015528"></a><a name="p18842182015528"></a>MindIE</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.950000000000001%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.4 " style="display:none"><p id="p10842162015220"><a name="p10842162015220"></a><a name="p10842162015220"></a>谢昌秀</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="7.5200000000000005%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.5 " style="display:none"><p id="p184272012521"><a name="p184272012521"></a><a name="p184272012521"></a>是</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row1741145514161"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="14.14%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.1 "><p id="p941195521618"><a name="p941195521618"></a><a name="p941195521618"></a><strong id="b1616672716374"><a name="b1616672716374"></a><a name="b1616672716374"></a>TTFT</strong></p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="64.96%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.2 "><p id="p44135511160"><a name="p44135511160"></a><a name="p44135511160"></a>Time to first token，首Token 延迟。LLM推理的一个指标。指从输入到输出第一个token的延迟。</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.510000000000001%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.3 " style="display:none"><p id="p2041105515164"><a name="p2041105515164"></a><a name="p2041105515164"></a>MindIE</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.950000000000001%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.4 " style="display:none"><p id="p5411755151613"><a name="p5411755151613"></a><a name="p5411755151613"></a>谢昌秀</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="7.5200000000000005%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.5 " style="display:none"><p id="p14411155151611"><a name="p14411155151611"></a><a name="p14411155151611"></a>是</p>
</td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>

</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="64.96%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.2 "><p id="p1488835112426"><a name="p1488835112426"></a><a name="p1488835112426"></a>灵衢总线。</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.42935706429357%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.3 " style="display:none"><p id="p1688815164215"><a name="p1688815164215"></a><a name="p1688815164215"></a>CANN</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.95930406959304%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.4 " style="display:none"><p id="p3888951104215"><a name="p3888951104215"></a><a name="p3888951104215"></a>臧燕</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="7.519248075192481%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.5 " style="display:none"><p id="p10888551134211"><a name="p10888551134211"></a><a name="p10888551134211"></a>是</p>
</td>

<tr id="row167635951817"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="14.14%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.1 "><p id="p156761459191814"><a name="p156761459191814"></a><a name="p156761459191814"></a><strong id="b1236010715199"><a name="b1236010715199"></a><a name="b1236010715199"></a>UDF</strong></p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="64.96%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.2 "><p id="p8676105920189"><a name="p8676105920189"></a><a name="p8676105920189"></a>User Defined Function，用户自定义功能。</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.42935706429357%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.3 " style="display:none"><p id="p2286163433813"><a name="p2286163433813"></a><a name="p2286163433813"></a>CANN</p>
</td>

</td>

</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row6222205801317"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="14.14%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.1 "><p id="p10843174095612"><a name="p10843174095612"></a><a name="p10843174095612"></a><strong id="b12843144015562"><a name="b12843144015562"></a><a name="b12843144015562"></a>UUID</strong></p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="64.96%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.2 "><p id="p19843134025618"><a name="p19843134025618"></a><a name="p19843134025618"></a>Universally Unique Identifier，通用唯一识别码。</p>
<p id="p784384075610"><a name="p784384075610"></a><a name="p784384075610"></a>通用唯一识别码是一个软件建构的标准，也是被开源软件基金会（Open Software Foundation, OSF）的组织应用在分布式计算环境（Distributed Computing Environment, DCE）领域的一部分。</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.42935706429357%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.3 " style="display:none"><p id="p102233584139"><a name="p102233584139"></a><a name="p102233584139"></a>MindCluster集群调度</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.95930406959304%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.4 " style="display:none"><p id="p1822314587131"><a name="p1822314587131"></a><a name="p1822314587131"></a>瞿铁铮</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="7.519248075192481%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.5 " style="display:none"><p id="p11473102610352"><a name="p11473102610352"></a><a name="p11473102610352"></a>是</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row119184319189"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="14.14%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.1 "><p id="p11875111718914"><a name="p11875111718914"></a><a name="p11875111718914"></a><strong id="b87637397184"><a name="b87637397184"></a><a name="b87637397184"></a>UT</strong></p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="64.96%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.2 "><p id="p1881810293911"><a name="p1881810293911"></a><a name="p1881810293911"></a>Unit Test，单元测试。</p>
<p id="p1167919317502"><a name="p1167919317502"></a><a name="p1167919317502"></a>单元测试是在软件开发过程中要进行的最低级别的测试活动，在单元测试活动中，软件的独立单元将在与程序的其他部分相隔离的情况下进行测试。</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.42935706429357%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.3 " style="display:none"><p id="p12469133711817"><a name="p12469133711817"></a><a name="p12469133711817"></a>MindStudio</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.95930406959304%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.4 " style="display:none"><p id="p1946943761814"><a name="p1946943761814"></a><a name="p1946943761814"></a>李翠</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="7.519248075192481%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.5 " style="display:none"><p id="p64739266358"><a name="p64739266358"></a><a name="p64739266358"></a>是，待上3ms术语库</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row8337181915114"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="14.14%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.1 "><p id="p13337419195114"><a name="p13337419195114"></a><a name="p13337419195114"></a><strong id="b4564102525113"><a name="b4564102525113"></a><a name="b4564102525113"></a>UDP</strong></p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="64.96%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.2 "><p id="p10337181918516"><a name="p10337181918516"></a><a name="p10337181918516"></a>User Datagram Protocol，用户数据报协议。</p>
<p id="p13691124174018"><a name="p13691124174018"></a><a name="p13691124174018"></a>在TCP/IP模型中，UDP为网络层以上和应用层以下提供了一个简单的接口。UDP只提供数据的不可靠传递，它一旦把应用程序发给网络层的数据发送出去，就不保留数据备份（所以UDP有时候也被认为是不可靠的数据报协议）。UDP在IP数据报的头部仅仅加入了复用和数据校验（字段）。</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.42935706429357%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.3 " style="display:none"><p id="p3337171955114"><a name="p3337171955114"></a><a name="p3337171955114"></a>HDK</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.95930406959304%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.4 " style="display:none"><p id="p1337121918514"><a name="p1337121918514"></a><a name="p1337121918514"></a>陈先雨</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="7.519248075192481%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.5 " style="display:none"><p id="p44730263356"><a name="p44730263356"></a><a name="p44730263356"></a>是</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row0154180124015"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="14.14%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.1 "><p id="p1486733912361"><a name="p1486733912361"></a><a name="p1486733912361"></a><strong id="b14514467369"><a name="b14514467369"></a><a name="b14514467369"></a>URMA</strong></p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="64.96%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.2 "><p id="p128671239193619"><a name="p128671239193619"></a><a name="p128671239193619"></a>Unified Remote Memory Access，统一远端内存访问。</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.42935706429357%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.3 " style="display:none"><p id="p18154209404"><a name="p18154209404"></a><a name="p18154209404"></a>CANN</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.95930406959304%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.4 " style="display:none"><p id="p3154150164013"><a name="p3154150164013"></a><a name="p3154150164013"></a>臧燕</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="7.519248075192481%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.5 " style="display:none"><p id="p1215418084019"><a name="p1215418084019"></a><a name="p1215418084019"></a>是</p>
</td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>

## W<a name="section7530173812529"></a>

<a name="table15964127165314"></a>
<table><thead align="left"><tr id="row18964074535"><th class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="14.14%" id="mcps1.1.6.1.1"><p id="p189647715533"><a name="p189647715533"></a><a name="p189647715533"></a><strong>术语/缩略语</strong></p>
</th>
<th class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="64.96%" id="mcps1.1.6.1.2"><p id="p596517185319"><a name="p596517185319"></a><a name="p596517185319"></a><strong>含义</strong></p>
</th>
<th class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.42935706429357%" id="mcps1.1.6.1.3" style="display:none"><p id="p159651576538"><a name="p159651576538"></a><a name="p159651576538"></a>领域</p>
</th>
<th class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.949305069493052%" id="mcps1.1.6.1.4" style="display:none"><p id="p59651072536"><a name="p59651072536"></a><a name="p59651072536"></a>负责人</p>
</th>
<th class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="7.529247075292471%" id="mcps1.1.6.1.5" style="display:none"><p id="p9380555143517"><a name="p9380555143517"></a><a name="p9380555143517"></a>是否确认一致</p>
</th>
</tr>
</thead>
<tbody><tr id="row178574571324"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="14.14%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.1 "><p id="p189099095513"><a name="p189099095513"></a><a name="p189099095513"></a><strong id="b81023593544"><a name="b81023593544"></a><a name="b81023593544"></a>Watchdog</strong></p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="64.96%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.2 "><p id="p1736113521545"><a name="p1736113521545"></a><a name="p1736113521545"></a>看门狗。</p>
<p id="p202751656124015"><a name="p202751656124015"></a><a name="p202751656124015"></a>一种硬件装置（通常是一个计时器或驱动器），用于监视连续运行的系统是否正常、功能是否发挥出来，这要通过与使用专用的设备驱动程序的系统软件进行通信来完成。</p>
<p id="p82752568400"><a name="p82752568400"></a><a name="p82752568400"></a>用于监控软件资源占用状态的定时计数器。在程序运行后启动看门狗，看门狗开始自动计数，程序需要定期清零该计数器，即所谓的喂狗，若超时不清零看门狗，则看门狗计数器溢出从而引起看门狗中断，造成系统复位。看门狗的作用就是防止程序发生死循环。</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.42935706429357%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.3 " style="display:none"><p id="p1136145217541"><a name="p1136145217541"></a><a name="p1136145217541"></a>HDK</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.949305069493052%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.4 " style="display:none"><p id="p136155213543"><a name="p136155213543"></a><a name="p136155213543"></a>陈先雨</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="7.529247075292471%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.5 " style="display:none"><p id="p93801555354"><a name="p93801555354"></a><a name="p93801555354"></a>是</p>
</td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>

</th>
</tr>
</thead>
<tbody><tr id="row66642214221"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="14.14%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.1 "><p id="p14216144714015"><a name="p14216144714015"></a><a name="p14216144714015"></a><strong id="b109011124162217"><a name="b109011124162217"></a><a name="b109011124162217"></a>业务面</strong></p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="64.96%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.2 "><p id="p1621619477015"><a name="p1621619477015"></a><a name="p1621619477015"></a>推理等业务接口所处的平面。</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.42935706429357%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.3 " style="display:none"><p id="p15970174118220"><a name="p15970174118220"></a><a name="p15970174118220"></a>MindIE</p>
</td>

<a name="table640412542712"></a>
<table><thead align="left"><tr id="row11404172552716"><th class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="14.14%" id="mcps1.1.6.1.1"><p id="p15037516299"><a name="p15037516299"></a><a name="p15037516299"></a><strong>术语/缩略语</strong></p>
</th>
<th class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="64.96%" id="mcps1.1.6.1.2"><p id="p9503155192920"><a name="p9503155192920"></a><a name="p9503155192920"></a><strong>含义</strong></p>
</th>

</th>
<th class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="7.519248075192481%" id="mcps1.1.6.1.5" style="display:none"><p id="p109550240361"><a name="p109550240361"></a><a name="p109550240361"></a>是否确认一致</p>

</thead>
<tbody><tr id="row4147412182219"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="14.14%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.1 "><p id="p185521126142510"><a name="p185521126142510"></a><a name="p185521126142510"></a><strong id="b31891610586"><a name="b31891610586"></a><a name="b31891610586"></a>ZeRO</strong></p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="64.96%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.2 "><p id="p255292602514"><a name="p255292602514"></a><a name="p255292602514"></a>Zero Redundancy Optimizer，零冗余优化器<strong id="b1263385462517"><a name="b1263385462517"></a><a name="b1263385462517"></a>。</strong></p>
<p id="p168997381546"><a name="p168997381546"></a><a name="p168997381546"></a>ZeRO是一种为了解决大规模分布式训练中的内存瓶颈问题而设计的优化器。它通过减少冗余数据来优化模型的内存使用，允许训练更大的模型。<span>该方法与传统的数据并行相比，内存效率得到了极大的提高，计算粒度和通信效率得到了保留。</span></p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.42935706429357%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.3 " style="display:none"><p id="p3261202592217"><a name="p3261202592217"></a><a name="p3261202592217"></a>MindCluster集群调度</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.95930406959304%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.4 " style="display:none"><p id="p17261925122210"><a name="p17261925122210"></a><a name="p17261925122210"></a>瞿铁铮</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="7.519248075192481%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.5 " style="display:none"><p id="p11955724173611"><a name="p11955724173611"></a><a name="p11955724173611"></a>是</p>
<p id="p7244181341412"><a name="p7244181341412"></a><a name="p7244181341412"></a>已在3MS术语库上提交更新</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row340552513275"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="14.14%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.1 "><p id="p6685581198"><a name="p6685581198"></a><a name="p6685581198"></a><strong id="b782613413510"><a name="b782613413510"></a><a name="b782613413510"></a>整网比对</strong></p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="64.96%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.2 "><p id="p168519801917"><a name="p168519801917"></a><a name="p168519801917"></a>network-wide comparison，精度比对工具里Tensor比对的一种方式。对网络模型中参与计算的所有算子进行精度比对。</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="6.42935706429357%" headers="mcps1.1.6.1.3 " style="display:none"><p id="p6233154533813"><a name="p6233154533813"></a><a name="p6233154533813"></a>MindStudio</p>
</td>

</td>

</td>
</tr>
